# Changelog

https://docs.botbonnie.appier.com/changelog

# Changelog

## [Introducing the Appier Enterprise Console (AEC)](/changelog/introducing-the-appier-enterprise-console-aec)

__ almost 2 years ago by Appier

We're excited to announce that starting this month, we're initiating a rollout of Appier's new, unified enterprise platform: the Appier Enterprise Console, or AEC for short. Aiming to provide a seamless and consistent user experience, AEC will serve as the centralized, cross-product management platform for Appier's full-funnel marketing solutions, including AIQUA, BotBonnie, AIRIS, and AIXON.

## [Single Sign-On](/changelog/single-sign-on)

 __ about 2 years ago by Appier

Starting from February 20, 2024, Single Sign-On (SSO) is applied to the following consoles, meaning that these consoles now share the same login credentials.

# Introducing The Appier Enterprise Console Aec

[Back to All](/changelog)

# Introducing the Appier Enterprise Console (AEC)

__ almost 2 years ago by Appier

We're excited to announce that starting this month, we're initiating a rollout of Appier's new, unified enterprise platform: the Appier Enterprise Console, or AEC for short. Aiming to provide a seamless and consistent user experience, AEC will serve as the centralized, cross-product management platform for Appier's full-funnel marketing solutions, including AIQUA, BotBonnie, AIRIS, and AIXON.

## 

A new, unified Appier console

You may have noticed that [Appier's omnichannel journey maps](doc:getting-started-with-journey-maps) feature isn't in the same console as your AIQUA account's settings and other AIQUA features like campaign, segment, and recommendation features. After your account migration to AEC, all AIQUA and BotBonnie features will be accessible from a single, unified console.

![](https://files.readme.io/18fbfdb-aec-sidebar.png)

Currently, AIQUA and BotBonnie accounts are eligible to migration to the new enterprise console. In future milestones, AIRIS and AIXON accounts will also be accessible from AEC, and migration for these products will be announced at a later date.

## 

Unified settings and account management

We're introducing shared settings and setup, such as channel integrations and account management, meaning you won't have to log in to AIQUA and BotBonnie separately, and channel integrations will be available for both product's features.

![](https://files.readme.io/db75149-aec-channels.png)

## 

Rollout timeline

The rollout for AEC begins in May 2024 and will progress over the next few quarters. Your customer success manager will provide you with details and specific timelines as your transition date approaches. No action is needed from you—we'll handle the console migration for you.



---

# Single Sign On

https://docs.botbonnie.appier.com/changelog/single-sign-on

[Back to All](/changelog)

# Single Sign-On

 __ about 2 years ago by Appier

Starting from February 20, 2024, Single Sign-On (SSO) is applied to the following consoles, meaning that these consoles now share the same login credentials.

  * AIQUA: <https://aiqua.appier.com/>
  * AIXON: <https://aixon.appier.com/>
  * Appier Enterprise Console: <https://console.appier.com/>
    * BotBonnie (Enterprise plan)
    * Journey Maps



> 👍
> 
> If you are accessing BotBonnie through `https://console.botbonnie.com`, this change does not affect you. SSO is currently only applied to the Appier Enterprise Console (`https://console.appier.com/`).

* * *

## 

Required actions

To avoid login issues, make sure you use the same user account to log into all consoles listed above.

If you have different login credentials for the consoles, please follow the instructions below.

  * **Same login email and password** : If you are using the same email and password for the consoles above, no action is needed.

  * **Same login email but different passwords** : If you are using the same email but different passwords to log in to the consoles, please use your AIQUA / AIXON password for all consoles listed above.

  * **Different login emails** : If you are using different emails to log in to the above consoles, grant Appier Enterprise Console access to your AIQUA / AIXON login account, and use your AIQUA / AIXON login account for all consoles above.

For example, if you are using `[[email protected]](/cdn-cgi/l/email-protection)` for AIQUA / AIXON and `[[email protected]](/cdn-cgi/l/email-protection)` for Appier Enterprise Console, you'll need to let `[[email protected]](/cdn-cgi/l/email-protection)` access Appier Enterprise Console by sending an invite.

To send an invite, log in to [Appier Enterprise Console](https://console.appier.com) , go to **Chat-BotBonnie** > **Settings** > **Team member** and click **Invite**. If you do not have admin access to Appier Enterprise Console, ask a colleague with admin access to send the invite to you.

![](https://files.readme.io/dc95b89-Screen_Shot_2024-03-13_at_7.26.24_PM.png)



> 📘
> 
> ### 
> 
> Note
> 
> Login email addresses are not case-sensitive, but it's recommended to use lower-case letters.



---

# Getting Started with BotBonnie

## 

Overview

BotBonnie, by Appier, is an omnichannel chatbot platform designed to streamline your marketing efforts and enhance customer engagement through conversational marketing. BotBonnie integrates seamlessly with various platforms to deliver consistent and personalized interactions. Whether you aim to boost sales, improve customer service, or gather valuable insights, BotBonnie is here to simplify and optimize your processes. Use BotBonnie for:

  * Conversational marketing
  * Automated customer service
  * Comprehensive customer segmentation
  * Analytics and reporting

  
![](https://files.readme.io/f16494f-IG_adv_kits.png)

* * *

## 

Conversational marketing

### 

No-code, drag-and-drop flow builder

Utilize BotBonnie's intuitive drag-and-drop interface to design your chatbot. You can create messages using various formats such as text, images, videos, and carousels.

![](https://files.readme.io/a8c75e4-bb-getting-started-flow.png)

### 

Comprehensive collection of prebuilt marketing kits

Customize campaigns using an array of pre-built gamified marketing tools such as daily check-ins, lucky draws, scratch-off cards, and member-get-member programs to engage interactions.

> 👍
> 
> ### 
> 
> Tip
> 
> Automate activity rewards, dynamic discount codes, and coupons to incentivize engagement and drive conversions. Use BotBonnie's advanced kits to easily manage sweepstakes, follower acquisition, surveys, and invoice registration to boost participation and grow your audience.

![](https://files.readme.io/986bee9-luck-wheel-kit-settings.png)

### 

Omnichannel messaging center

BotBonnie supports integrations with your business's social media and messaging accounts including LINE, Facebook Messenger, Instagram, WhatsApp, KakaoTalk, Viber, and Zalo.

In addition, you can connect to your e-commerce platforms of choice, including Shopify, Shopline, 91 APP, and Cyberbiz, to send notifications for post-purchase journeys.

> 👍
> 
> ### 
> 
> Tip
> 
> Connect multiple LINE accounts and Facebook pages to a single bot to streamline operations and centralize communication. Filter customer support conversations by user nickname, tags, and message types, and respond to them on mobile just like chatting with friends for a more personal touch.

![](https://files.readme.io/e00d821-bb-getting-started-live-chat.png)

* * *

## 

Automated customer service

Configure proactive chat prompts to start conversations with visitors at the right moment, reducing bounce rates and encouraging conversions.

  * Growth tools allow you to acquire new customers from offline events, such as providing a QR code that users can scan during an in-person promotional event to add your brand's official account.
  * Broadcasts allow you to send important announcements, such as sales events, to your customers.
  * Automated responses and seamless handovers to human customer service agents ensure continuous and efficient support.



* * *

## 

Comprehensive customer segmentation

Collect and segment user data based on their interactions and behavior. Target these segments with personalized messages to maximize engagement and marketing returns.

> 👍
> 
> ### 
> 
> Tip
> 
> Collect user interaction data and profiles to enhance personalized services and improve customer satisfaction. BotBonnie allows you to efficiently manage user lists for ad placement and data integration to optimize marketing campaigns and drive better results.

* * *

## 

Analytics and reporting

Connect and unify data from various touchpoints (messaging apps, websites, e-commerce platforms) and use Appier’s AI models to analyze data and generate insightful reports.

![](https://files.readme.io/6d7d5b1-bb-getting-started-analytics.png)

* * *

Ready to start setting up your chatbot? Follow our [quick start guide](doc:quick-start-guide) for a step-by-step setup instructions.

__Updated 12 months ago

* * *

[Quick Start Guide](/docs/quick-start-guide)

# Account Linking

> 👍
> 
> ### 
> 
> Supported channels
> 
>   * LINE
>   * Facebook
> 


Use the account linking kit to establish a mapping between a user's account on the connected platform, such as LINE or Facebook, with their BotBonnie profile.

With account linking, you can:

  * Create user segments based on a user's account linking status
  * Enrich a user's profile by adding their ID from your customer relationship management platform, e.g. for enhanced customer service and support and filtering



After you've established a link between the user in your CRM and the user in BotBonnie, you'll be able to see the user's CRM ID in their profile in the Personalization Cloud console under **Audience > User list**:

![](https://files.readme.io/0dc865a-account-id.png)

To reflect a user's new linking status when linking or unlinking an account via API, you can also choose to optionally update the following details associated with the BotBonnie user:

  * Tags
  * Parameters
  * Assigned menu groups (LINE menus, Messenger menus)



* * *

## 

1\. Create a login page to collect account linking details

Work with your developers to implement a login page for account linking—this is the page that will be opened by the account linking kit. The login page is required to:

  * Collect the details required to link the account, i.e. the user's ID

  * Receive URL parameters that will be to sent to BotBonnie to authorize the account linking process


![Create a login page for users who want to link their account.](https://files.readme.io/1d21c28-example-login-page.png)

Create a login page for users who want to link their account.

* * *

## 

2\. Prepare account linking parameters

When the user navigates to the account linking page, you'll need to:

  * Extract BotBonnie account linking parameters from the query string of the login page. The parameters differ depending on the messaging platform you're using:
    * LINE account linking parameters
    * Messenger account linking parameters
  * Retrieve the user's ID in your CRM. This is a required account linking parameter.
  * Add the user's messaging platform ID (i.e. LINE user ID or Messenger ID) to your database.



### 

LINE account linking parameters

Name| Description  
---|---  
`carry`| An account linking parameter contained in the login page URL's query string.Note: `carry` must be extracted from the login page URL and appended to the account linking success page's URL.  
`linkToken`| An account linking parameter contained in the login page URL's query string.Note: `linkToken` must be extracted from the login page URL and appended to the account linking success page's URL. This parameter is only valid for 10 minutes.  
`nonce`| The user's unique ID in your CRM. Must be 10-255 characters.  
  
### 

Messenger account linking parameters

Name| Description  
---|---  
`carry`| An account linking parameter contained in the login page URL's query string.Note: `carry` must be extracted from the login page URL and appended to the account linking success page's URL.  
`redirect_uri`| An account linking parameter contained in the login page URL's query string.Note: `redirect_uri` must be extracted from the login page URL and appended to the account linking success page's URL.  
`account_linking_token`| An account linking parameter contained in the login page URL's query string.Note: `account_linking_token` must be extracted from the login page URL and appended to the account linking success page's URL.  
`authorization_code`| The user's unique ID in your CRM. Must be 10-255 characters.  
  
* * *

## 

3\. Redirect to the account linking success page

Once the user has entered their details in your login page, redirect the user to the account linking success page and pass the account linking parameters in the URL.

The redirect URL must include all account linking parameters in its query string for BotBonnie to update the user's profile. Once the page is loaded, BotBonnie will extract the parameters from the query string to complete the process.

Messaging platform| Account linking success page URL  
---|---  
LINE| `https://rd.botbonnie.com/account/link?carry=<CARRY>&linkToken=<LINKTOKEN>&nonce=<NONCE>`  
Messenger| `https://rd.botbonnie.com/account/link?carry=<CARRY>&redirect_uri=<REDIRECT_URI>&account_linking_token=<ACCOUNT_LINKING_TOKEN>&authorization_code=<AUTHORIZATION_CODE>`  
  
* * *

## 

4\. Add the account linking kit to your chatbot flow

From the left menu, go to **Flow** , then click **Advanced kits** and select **Account linking**.

![](https://files.readme.io/9a8296e-nav-account-linking.png)

  1. Open the kit and click **Start editing** to open the account linking kit sub-flow.
  2. In the module titled **Start account linking** , click the button used to initiate the account linking process (the default name is **Link now**), then set the following settings:
     1. **Button name** : Input a button label. This is visible to the user.
     2. **On click** : Paste the URL of your login page.

![](https://files.readme.io/e95cc4a-account-linking-kit-login-page-url.png)

  3. (Optional) In the module titled **Result conditions #2** and **Linked successfully** , you can set a custom reply and triggered actions upon a successful account linking.

![](https://files.readme.io/d9ff408-account-linking-successful-modules.png)

  4. Deploy your changes.



__Updated 6 months ago

* * *

[Scratch-Off](/docs/scratch-off-kit)[MGM](/docs/mgm-kit)

# Add Journey Nodes

## 

Overview

Each node in the journey map is a step in the user journey, and each node contains settings related to its specific purpose. For example, a message node contains campaign creative settings, while a split node contains logic for splitting users into different paths.

The first step in creating your journey is to add an [entry trigger node](doc:entry-trigger-node), which specifies how users enter the journey. Next, continue customizing your journey by:

  * Adding nodes
  * Editing nodes
  * Copying nodes
  * Deleting nodes
  * Undoing and redoing changes



* * *

## 

Add nodes

To add a node, click on the **+** that appears in the journey map. After clicking the add button, you'll be able to select one of the following node types:

  * **Entry trigger node** : Specify how users will enter the journey. Only available as the first node in the journey. For details, see [Entry Trigger Node](doc:entry-trigger-node).
  * **Message** : Reach out to users across different channels. In a message node, you can set up the creative you want to send. For details, see [Message Node](doc:message-node).
  * **Split** : Specify a condition that splits users into different paths. For details, see [Split Node](doc:split-node).
  * **Wait** : Wait for a certain amount of time before continuing to the next node. For details, see [Wait Node](doc:wait-node).
  * **Update users** : Update user-related information. For details, see [Update Users Node](doc:update-users-node).

![](https://files.readme.io/99e397c-jm-add-node.png)

* * *

## 

Edit nodes

To edit a node, simply click on it, and its settings panel will open on the right. Then, you can modify its settings and click **Save** to apply the changes.

![](https://files.readme.io/f30020e-jm-edit-existing-node.png)

### 

Comparison operators

Some nodes support comparison operators that evaluate user events and attributes. For example:

  * **Wait for condition** nodes allow you pause users at a specific step in the journey until they satisfy a condition, such as completing a `checkout_completed` event for a product with an ID equal to `123`.
  * **Check condition** nodes allow you to split users to different paths depending on the conditions they satisfy, for example, splitting users to different paths based on their membership level.



Different comparison operators are available for strings and numbers.

![](https://files.readme.io/92acc7b2fc1d34ffec20f824fa2441232a629cf0cbd604ad14df08b67c2de9df-comparison-operators.gif)

#### 

String comparison operators

Operator| Usage| Example  
---|---|---  
**is one of**|  True if the operand is an exact match of at least one of the specified values.Specifying multiple comma-separated values is supported.| `deviceType` is one of `mobile`, `tablet`  
**equals**|  True if the operand matches the specified value exactly.| `product_id` equals `A123`  
**exists**|  True if the operand exists, regardless of its value. Note that empty values also evaluate to true.| `custom_attribute` exists  
**contains**|  True if the operand contains the specified value.You can only specify one value to compare—specifying multiple values is not supported.| `product_category` contains `electronics`  
**matches regex**|  True if the operand matches the specified regular expression.Regular expressions are patterns used to match character combinations in strings.| `email` matches regex `^\[a-zA-Z0-9.\_%+-]+@(example.org\|example.com)$`  
  
#### 

Numerical comparison operators

Operator| Usage  
---|---  
`≥`| True if the operand is greater than or equal to the specified value.  
`≤`| True if the operand is less than or equal to the specified value.  
`>`| True if the operand is greater than the specified value.  
`<`| True if the operand is less than the specified value.  
`=`| True if the operand is equal to the specified value.  
`exists`| True if the operand exists, regardless of its value.  
`between`| True if the operand falls within the specified range (inclusive).  
  
* * *

## 

Delete nodes

To delete a node, hover over it and click the trash can.

![](https://files.readme.io/44e6169-jm-delete-node1.png)

Next, choose how to handle the path after the node you're deleting, then click **Confirm**.

The available deletion options will differ depending on the node type. For example, if you're deleting a message node, you can choose whether to delete the path for reachable users, the path for non-reachable users, or both.

![](https://files.readme.io/c2b3777-jm-delete-node2.png)

* * *

## 

Copy nodes

To copy a node and its subsequent branches, hover over the node and click on the copy button. After clicking the copy button, you can copy the node into any of the available positions shown in the journey.

![](https://files.readme.io/7d1e336-jm-copy-node.png)

* * *

## 

Undo and redo changes

In the bottom right corner of the canvas, you'll find undo and redo buttons, which allow you to revert or reapply the following operations:

  * Adding a node
  * Deleting a node
  * Copying and pasting a node

![](https://files.readme.io/4d6b07f-jm-undo-redo.png)

__Updated 9 months ago

* * *

# Random Reply

The random reply kit can be used in campaigns or games where you want to randomly show a different module to users each time. Using this kit, you can:

  * Add multiple modules and BotBonnie will randomly display one module to the user
  * You can set the probability for each reply.



### 

Example use case

Here are some examples of how the random reply kit can be used.

  * Run a fortune-telling game by having each module be a different fortune result
  * Conduct a random prize draw by setting each module to be a different prize



* * *

## 

Random Reply kit setup

### 

1\. Add the kit to your flow

From the left menu, go to **Flow** , then click **Advanced kits** and select **Random reply**.

![](https://files.readme.io/bbce059-nav-random-reply.png)

Click **Start editing** or double-click the kit to open the kit.

![](https://files.readme.io/f2f0950-random-reply-start-editing.png)

### 

2\. Add message modules

Next, click **Add module** to create some modules. From these modules, BotBonnie will randomly display one to users.

![](https://files.readme.io/a1de1e6-Screen_Shot_2023-08-18_at_7.00.25_PM.png)

### 

3\. Set the probability

Click **Settings** and set the probability for each module. You can also click **Divide evenly** to divide the probability equally among the modules.

![](https://files.readme.io/41a44bd-Screen_Shot_2023-08-18_at_7.04.00_PM.png)

### 

4\. Connect the kit to the rest of the flow

Go back to the Flows page, and connect the random reply kit to the rest of the flow.

![](https://files.readme.io/9607d47-Screen_Shot_2023-09-06_at_4.35.35_PM.png)

Powered by [](https://readme.com?ref_src=hub&project=botbonnie-resource-center)

# Auto Reply for Post Comments

You can use BotBonnie's auto-reply feature to automatically respond to users who comment on your posts with comment replies or private messages. You can set the conditions that need to be met for auto-replies to be triggered.

For example, you can use auto replies to encourage users to tag their friends in the comment by providing a discount code by:

  1. Creating an auto reply that sends a message containing a discount code.
  2. Adding a condition to send an auto reply to users who tag their friends in a comment on your post.



After setting up the auto reply, users who fulfill this condition will receive an auto reply from your page containing the discount code.

* * *

## 

Create Facebook auto replies

### 

1\. Add an auto-reply as a growth tool

Go **Growth tools** , then click **\+ Add growth tool**.

![](https://files.readme.io/6f09a413d88756340f56cb64ddb60296cb00bb1189d02a6cb89b425eb581a778-facebook-growth-tools.png)

Select **Facebook** and choose one of the following options:

  * **Facebook Auto reply for post comments** : Create an auto reply for one post on your connected page.
  * **Facebook Multiple Auto Reply** : Create an auto reply for multiple posts on your connected page by providing post URLs.



> 👍
> 
> ### 
> 
> Tip
> 
> Use **Facebook Multiple Auto Reply** if you'd like to use the same auto reply for multiple posts.

![](https://files.readme.io/1cfea01-fb-auto-reply-choose-growth-too.png)

### 

2\. Name the auto reply

Enter a name to help you identify the auto reply.

![](https://files.readme.io/8cef7ef-fb-auto-reply-name.png)

### 

3\. Set an operation period

Set an operation period for the auto reply. There are two options:

  * **Permanent** : This auto reply will be active immediately and continue to run without an end date.
  * **Limited-time** : This auto reply will only be active during the time period you specified.

![](https://files.readme.io/31b3dcf-fb-auto-reply-operation-period.png)

### 

4\. Select a post or specify post URLs

Depending on whether you chose **Facebook Auto reply for post comments** or **Facebook Multiple Auto Reply** when creating the growth tool, the way you associate an auto reply with a post or multiple posts will differ.

  * **Facebook auto reply for post comments**: Select a post from a connected account to associate with the auto reply.
  * **Facebook multiple auto reply**: Provide multiple post URLs to associate with the auto reply.



#### 

Facebook auto reply for post comments

Under **Facebook Posts** , select the connected page from the dropdown, then click **\+ Select post**.

![](https://files.readme.io/5c1d2cf-fb-auto-reply-single-post-select-page.png)

From the list of available posts, select the one you'd like to associate with the auto reply, then click **Confirm**.

> 📘
> 
> ### 
> 
> Scheduled posts
> 
> Note that scheduled posts won't appear in the selection screen until after they're published.

![](https://files.readme.io/75e47a0-fb-auto-reply-single-post-select-post.png)

#### 

Facebook multiple auto reply

Under **Facebook post link** , enter the URLs of the posts you'd like to associate with the growth tool. For URLs to be valid, the post must be from a connected Facebook page.

![](https://files.readme.io/eed55a6-fb-auto-reply-multiple-posts.png)

### 

5\. Set up reply conditions

Click **\+ Add default reply** or **\+ Add conditional reply**.

  * **Default reply** : Auto replies will be triggered by all comments made under this post.
  * **Conditional reply** : Auto replies will only be triggered by comments that meet the conditions you set. You can create multiple conditional replies if needed.

![](https://files.readme.io/d356a50-Screen_Shot_2023-06-02_at_4.06.11_PM.png)

#### 

Setting up conditional reply

A condition reply allows you to set conditions based on the number of friends tagged or based on whether the user's comment includes the keywords you specified.

  * **Number of friends tagged** : Select a number to indicate the number of people the user needs to tag in the comment. If you set the number to `2`, the user needs to tag two or more friends in the comment to meet the condition.

![](https://files.readme.io/813407f-Screen_Shot_2023-06-02_at_5.08.57_PM.png)

  * **Comment** : Enter one or more keywords, then select a matching option from the dropdown. Depending on the matching option you select, the user's comment will need to include one or all keywords you entered.
    * **Contains any keywords** : The user's comment needs to include at least one keyword and can include other text.
    * **Contains all keywords** : The user's comment needs to include all keywords and can include other text.
    * **Exact match** : The user's comment needs to be an exact match with one of the keywords and cannot include other text.

![](https://files.readme.io/da43fe6-Screen_Shot_2023-06-02_at_1.59.25_PM.png)

The following table provides examples of what types of user input would match for two keywords, `I want` and `free sample`, based on different settings.

Matching option| Examples  
---|---  
`Contains any keywords`| If the user's comment is: • `I want` → Match • `free sample` → Match • `I want to get a free sample`→ Match • `sample` → Not a match  
`Contains all keywords`| If the user's comment is: • `I want free sample` → Match • `I want to get free sample` → Match • `free sample` → Not a match  
`Exact match`| If the user's comment is: • `I want` → Match • `free sample` → Match • `I want free sample` → Not a match • `I want to get free sample` → Not a match  
  
### 

6\. Create reply modules

You can add two different types of reply modules:

  * Select **Add public reply** to reply to users in public comments.
  * Select **Add private reply** to reply in private messages.



You can also add both public and private replies if needed. For example:

  * In the public reply, notify users who commented users know that a private message has been sent to them.
  * In the private reply, you send the actual discount code.

![](https://files.readme.io/49ecda1-Screen_Shot_2023-06-02_at_4.25.27_PM.png) ![](https://files.readme.io/00aa238-Screen_Shot_2023-06-02_at_4.27.57_PM.png)

#### 

Multiple reply modules

  * If you add more than one reply module under a reply type, BotBonnie will randomly send one reply module to the user.
  * If you have multiple reply modules under both public and private replies, you can decide if you want to lock the public and private replies in pairs.
  * If you enable **Send public and private replies in pairs** , the first public reply will always be sent together with the first private reply, and the second row of replies will always be sent together, etc. You will need to have the same number of reply modules under public and private replies.



__Updated 6 months ago

# Auto Reply for Reels Comment

With BotBonnie's Reels comment auto-reply feature, you can automatically respond to comments on your Instagram Reels. Easily send both public replies and personal messages to your followers based on conditions you set.

### 

Example use case: Offer rewards for comments and tags

Encourage your followers to tag friends or leave a comment by offering special rewards. When they meet your conditions, you can send them a public reply directing them to check their inbox, while also sending them a private message containing their exclusive discount code.

![](https://files.readme.io/57462f1c3043826aeac2b5b3cd314c25c4253ba102ed0ad0e80ca97d2984f916-reels-comment-reply.png)

### 

Prerequisite

[Connect your Instagram business account](doc:connecting-instagram-account-to-botbonnie#3-connect-your-instagram-account-to-botbonnie) to your BotBonnie bot.

* * *

## 

Set up auto-reply for Reels comments

### 

1\. Add an auto-reply as a growth tool

In the left menu, click **Growth tools** , then click **\+ Add growth tool**. Next, go to **Instagram** tab and select **Reels comment reply**.

![](https://files.readme.io/46283824afcdcb158be79875696a6d23b78d7723839def283c34456da5abd6f2-add-growth-tool.png)

### 

2\. Name the auto-reply

Enter a name to help you identify the auto-reply, such as the name of the campaign.

![](https://files.readme.io/b89da20cee7bf9dfdd065d5403b833fff5381d48b5c2bc3e60969502a098a858-campaign-name.png)

### 

3\. Set an operation period

Set an operation period for the auto-reply. There are two options:

  * **Permanent** : This auto-reply will be applied immediately and continue to run without an end date.
  * **Limited-time** : This auto-reply will only be applied during the specified time period.

![](https://files.readme.io/dc8742b606d3bf12f56e3184267e41ab9a31d3268a7adff45426a54454e4005a-operation-period.png)

### 

4\. Select the Instagram account and Reel

Select the Instagram business account from the dropdown, then click **\+ Select Reel** to choose the reel you want to configure for auto-replies. Click **Confirm** to save your selection.

![](https://files.readme.io/36c7cc47ffa0687266010f1ffcd87b4d3fd59cf3f9d00b4f2bbdcf432e999646-select-reel.png)

### 

5\. Set up reply conditions

Click **+Add default reply** or **+Add conditional reply**.

  * **Default reply** : Auto-replies will be triggered by all comments under this reel.
  * **Conditional reply** : Auto-replies will only be triggered by comments that meet the conditions you set. You can set up multiple conditional replies if needed.

![](https://files.readme.io/f67ee234ffafbe7ffcebc747a3f683161591e91654c99f45293aa3596ffc77b8-default-conditional-reply.png)

#### 

Set up conditional reply

If you selected **Add conditional reply** , you can set the conditions based on the number of friends tagged or whether the user's comment includes specified keywords.

  * **Comments** : Enter one or multiple keywords and select a matching option:
    * **Contains any keywords** : The comment must include at least one keyword.
    * **Contains all keywords** : The comment must include all specified keywords.
    * **Exact match** : The comment must exactly match one of the keywords.
  * **Number of friends tagged** : Select a number to indicate how many people the user needs to tag in the comment.

![](https://files.readme.io/f11ec7b577b6e4b5526944fb480592283f0e0cda76909cb4e2616ef2e2e1a915-condition-rules.png)

### 

6\. Create reply modules

You can select **\+ Add public reply** to respond to users in public comments or **\+ Add private reply** to send a direct message.

![](https://files.readme.io/33c0d558452117432ab3ef3e381750f09b9e2647327239f146503c8b54719c21-reply-messages.png)

#### 

Multiple reply modules

If you create multiple reply modules under the same reply type (public or private), a random reply module will be sent when the condition is met.

To ensure each public reply is paired with a specific private reply, enable **Send public and private replies in pairs**.

![](https://files.readme.io/70e25f246a9d609e96bee5aa9a2dfd927187cea735ae2ecb6341c82a4d185c11-replies-in-pairs.png)

Powered by [](https://readme.com?ref_src=hub&project=botbonnie-resource-center)

# Auto Reply for Live Comments

You can use BotBonnie's auto reply for live comments to automatically send a private message to users who comment during your live broadcast. Using conditional replies, a different auto reply can be sent to users based on what the users write in their comments.

This allows you to make the live broadcast more interactive without having to manually reply to each user.

![](https://files.readme.io/9f08df7-Screen_Shot_2022-11-28_at_7.01.33_PM.png)

> 📘
> 
> ### 
> 
> Note
> 
>   * Make sure your Instagram business account is linked to your BotBonnie bot.
>   * Auto replies can only be sent as private messages to users who commented during the live stream.
>   * If a user repeatedly makes the exact same comment during the live broadcast, Instagram might filter out the comment. The user might not receive the auto reply when this happens.
> 


* * *

## 

Set up auto reply for live comments

### 

1\. Add an auto reply growth tool

In the left menu, click **Growth tools** , then click **\+ Add growth tool**.

![](https://files.readme.io/ca4e1d6-growth-tool-nav.png)

Select **Instagram** and click **Auto reply for live comments**.

![](https://files.readme.io/42585c1-ig-auto-reply-live-comment-add.png)

### 

2\. Name the auto reply

Enter a name to help you identify the auto reply (e.g. the name of the campaign).

![](https://files.readme.io/51c6f0a-Screen_Shot_2023-06-02_at_2.33.06_PM.png)

### 

3\. Select the Instagram account and live broadcast

Select your Instagram business account, click **Select live** , select the ongoing live broadcast, and click **Confirm**.

![](https://files.readme.io/6d8b792-Screen_Shot_2023-06-02_at_2.34.33_PM.png)

### 

4\. Set up reply conditions

Click **Add default reply** or **Add conditional reply**.

  * **Default reply** : Auto-replies will be triggered by all comments made during this live stream.
  * **Conditional reply** : Auto-replies will only be triggered by comments that meet the conditions you set. You can set up multiple conditional replies if needed.

![](https://files.readme.io/dc8dea6-Screen_Shot_2023-06-02_at_2.34.33_PM.png)

#### 

Setting up conditional reply

If you have selected **Add conditional reply** , you can set the conditions based on the number of friends tagged or based on whether the user's comment includes the keywords you specified.

  * **Number of friends tagged** : Select a number to indicate the number of people the user needs to tag in the comment. If you set the number to `3`, the user needs to tag three or more friends in the comment to meet the condition.

![](https://files.readme.io/0e85032-Screen_Shot_2023-06-02_at_1.58.12_PM.png)

  * **Comments** : Enter one or multiple keywords, and select a matching option. Depending on the matching option you select, the user's comment will need to include one or all keywords you entered.
    * **Contains any keywords** : The user's comment needs to include at least one keyword and can include other text.
    * **Contains all keywords** : The user's comment needs to include all keywords and can include other text.
    * **Exact match** : The user's comment needs to be an exact match with one of the keywords and cannot include other text.



Let's say you entered 2 keywords, `I want` and `free sample`. Refer to the table below to see examples of what would be considered a match under the different matching options.

![](https://files.readme.io/317602f-Screen_Shot_2023-06-02_at_1.59.25_PM.png)

Matching option| Examples  
---|---  
`Contains any keywords`| If the user's comment is: • `I want` → match • `free sample` → match • `I want to get a free sample`→ match • `sample` → not a match  
`Contains all keywords`| If the user's comment is: • `I want free sample` → match • `I want to get free sample` → match • `free sample` → not a match  
`Exact match`| If the user's comment is: • `I want` → match • `free sample` → match • `I want free sample` → not a match • `I want to get free sample` → not a match  
  
### 

5\. Create reply modules

Click **Add private reply** and create the reply module.

![](https://files.readme.io/ec49c99-Screen_Shot_2023-06-02_at_2.02.13_PM.png)

> 📘
> 
> ### 
> 
> Note
> 
> If you add more than one reply module under the default or conditional reply, BotBonnie will randomly send one reply module to the user.

# Auto Reply for Messenger Ads (JSON)

Messenger Ads JSON helps you attract new users and engage existing ones by integrating BotBonnie’s chatbot flows with your Messenger ads. This feature allows you to use pre-built modules and responses from BotBonnie when setting up ads in Meta Ads Manager. When users click the ad’s call-to-action button, they receive an opt-in message and are directed to Messenger to start a conversation with your chatbot.

* * *

## 

Set up an auto-reply for Messenger ads

To use a BotBonnie module in a Messenger Ad, you need to obtain its JSON string. For detailed instructions, see [Meta's guide to creating Messenger Ads with your existing BotBonnie message flow](https://www.facebook.com/business/help/2240216809588709).

> 👍
> 
> ### 
> 
> Recommendation
> 
> For best results, set your campaign objective to **Engagement** when using Messenger Ads with BotBonnie.

### 

Get JSON Ads string

  1. Navigate to **Growth tools** and click **\+ Add growth tool**.
  2. Select **Facebook** > **Messenger Ads JSON**.

![](https://files.readme.io/a3da8a7939fc081a06278afd074a2950c9e8a95ee60845f5db696f9d72043bc5-messenger-ads-json.png)

  3. From the dropdown, select a module for the first message to send in the Messenger.

![](https://files.readme.io/2e517f5cd23791ebd38df3aa2b0e08c8917857d556946642ce6d0f8e3e285499-module-dropdown.png)

  4. Click the copy icon next to the JSON string box.

![](https://files.readme.io/8e27a635ea6053ddf774f3099d39b7f0f15850d8c2ce825384b9e1024de2a4b6-json-copied.png)

  5. In [Meta Ads Manager](https://adsmanager.facebook.com/adsmanager/), paste the JSON string into the **Enter JSON code** field to link your BotBonnie flow to the ad.



> 📘
> 
> ### 
> 
> Note
> 
> If you need help completing the setup, see [Meta’s guide to setting up Messenger Ads with JSON](https://www.facebook.com/business/help/2240216809588709).

![](https://files.readme.io/6f5bfaaa2abf0e7e91d221ef80a21fee01444120defb0d69a7726f3b737d1c09-enter-json-code.png)

> 📘
> 
> ### 
> 
> Note
> 
> Remove all backslashes () from the copied JSON string before using it.
> 
> ![](https://files.readme.io/03f5306f3b485bad3026e7a5b28576b64f73b11d8a7318697e8329477b278ef5-remove-slashes.png)

# Auto Reply for Post Comments

You can use BotBonnie's auto-reply feature for post comments to automatically send public and private messages to users who comment on your posts. You can set the conditions that need to be met for auto-replies to be triggered.

For example, you can encourage users to tag their friends in the comment by providing a reward. When the users meet the requirement by tagging their friends, BotBonnie automatically leaves a public reply asking users to check their inbox for the discount code, and a private reply is sent to the users to provide the actual discount code.

![](https://files.readme.io/c2edbd6-Screen_Shot_2022-12-09_at_4.43.32_PM.png)

> 📘
> 
> ### 
> 
> Note
> 
> Make sure your Instagram business account is linked to your BotBonnie bot.

* * *

## 

Set up auto-reply for post comment

### 

1\. Add an auto-reply as a growth tool

In the left menu, click **Growth tools** , then click **\+ Add growth tool**.

![](https://files.readme.io/ca4e1d6-growth-tool-nav.png)

Select **Instagram** and click **Auto reply for post comments**.

![](https://files.readme.io/c86b325-ig-auto-reply-add.png)

### 

2\. Name the auto-reply

Enter a name to help you identify the auto-reply (e.g. the name of the campaign).

![](https://files.readme.io/9bddf5d-Screen_Shot_2023-06-02_at_4.05.21_PM.png)

### 

3\. Set an operation period

Set an operation period for the auto-reply. There are two options:

  * **Permanent** : This auto-reply will be applied immediately and continue to run without an end date.
  * **Limited-time** : This auto-reply will only be applied during the time period you specified.

![](https://files.readme.io/f06d268-Screen_Shot_2022-11-29_at_5.48.47_PM.png)

### 

4\. Select the Instagram account and post

Select the Instagram business account, click **Select post** , select the post you want to add the auto-reply to, and click **Confirm**.

![](https://files.readme.io/64c6228-Screen_Shot_2023-06-02_at_3.55.53_PM.png) ![](https://files.readme.io/1b26d03-Screen_Shot_2023-06-02_at_4.00.53_PM.png)

### 

5\. Set up reply conditions

Click **Add default reply** or **Add conditional reply**.

  * **Default reply** : Auto-replies will be triggered by all comments made under this post.
  * **Conditional reply** : Auto-replies will only be triggered by comments that meet the conditions you set. You can set up multiple conditional replies if needed.

![](https://files.readme.io/d356a50-Screen_Shot_2023-06-02_at_4.06.11_PM.png)

#### 

Set up conditional reply

If you have selected **Add conditional reply** , you can set the conditions based on the number of friends tagged or based on whether the user's comment includes the keywords you specified.

  * **Number of friends tagged** : Select a number to indicate the number of people the user needs to tag in the comment. If you set the number to `2`, the user needs to tag two or more friends in the comment to meet the condition.

![](https://files.readme.io/813407f-Screen_Shot_2023-06-02_at_5.08.57_PM.png)

  * **Comments** : Enter one or multiple keywords, and select a matching option. Depending on the matching option you select, the user's comment will need to include one or all keywords you entered.
    * **Contains any keywords** : The user's comment needs to include at least one keyword and can include other text.
    * **Contains all keywords** : The user's comment needs to include all keywords and can include other text.
    * **Exact match** : The user's comment needs to be an exact match with one of the keywords and cannot include other text.



Let's say you entered 2 keywords, `I want` and `free sample`. Refer to the table below to see examples of what would be considered a match under the different matching options.

![](https://files.readme.io/da43fe6-Screen_Shot_2023-06-02_at_1.59.25_PM.png)

Matching option| Examples  
---|---  
`Contains any keywords`| If the user's comment is: • `I want` → match • `free sample` → match • `I want to get a free sample`→ match • `sample` → not a match  
`Contains all keywords`| If the user's comment is: • `I want free sample` → match • `I want to get free sample` → match • `free sample` → not a match  
`Exact match`| If the user's comment is: • `I want` → match • `free sample` → match • `I want free sample` → not a match • `I want to get free sample` → not a match  
  
### 

6\. Create reply modules

You can select **Add public reply** to reply to users in public comments, or select **Add private reply** to reply in private messages.

You can set up both public and private replies if needed. For example, in the public reply, you can let the commented users know that a private message has been sent to them. In the private reply, you can then include the actual discount code.

![](https://files.readme.io/49ecda1-Screen_Shot_2023-06-02_at_4.25.27_PM.png) ![](https://files.readme.io/00aa238-Screen_Shot_2023-06-02_at_4.27.57_PM.png)

Here's what the public and private replies look like to users.

![](https://files.readme.io/7156ff4-Screen_Shot_2022-12-09_at_4.34.15_PM.png)

#### 

Multiple reply modules

If you add more than one reply module under a reply type, BotBonnie will randomly send one reply module to the user.

If you have multiple reply modules under both public and private replies, you can decide if you want to lock the public and private replies in pairs.

By selecting **Send public and private replies in pairs** , the first public reply will always be sent together with the first private reply, and the second row of replies will always be sent together, etc. You will need to have the same number of reply modules under public and private replies.

![](https://files.readme.io/df9e3cc-Screen_Shot_2023-06-02_at_4.13.32_PM.png)

# Auto Reply for Story Mentions

You can use BotBonnie's auto reply feature to automatically send a private message to users who mention your Instagram business account in their Stories. This feature is commonly used to automate campaigns that encourage users to promote a brand, and rewards can be automatically provided to users using the auto reply.

![](https://files.readme.io/01ddb23-Screen_Shot_2022-11-17_at_3.56.02_PM.png)

> 📘
> 
> ### 
> 
> Note
> 
>   * Make sure your Instagram business account is linked to your BotBonnie bot.
>   * Auto reply for story mention will only be triggered if the user's Instagram account is set to public. You can remind users to set their Instagram account to public.
> 


* * *

## 

Set up auto reply for story mentions

### 

1\. Add an auto reply as a growth tool

In the left menu, click **Growth tools** , then click **\+ Add growth tool**.

![](https://files.readme.io/ca4e1d6-growth-tool-nav.png)

Select **Instagram** and click **Auto reply for Story mentions**.

![](https://files.readme.io/2d1fd0f-ig-auto-reply-story-mention-add.png)

### 

2\. Complete basic settings

Enter a name to help you identify the auto reply (e.g. the name of the campaign) and select your Instagram business account.

![](https://files.readme.io/85037ee-Screen_Shot_2022-11-17_at_12.01.51_PM.png)

### 

3\. Set an operation period

Set an operation period for the auto-reply. There are two options:

  * **Permanent** : This auto-reply will be applied immediately and continue to run without an end date.
  * **Limited-time** : This auto-reply will only be applied during the time period you specified.

![](https://files.readme.io/c482cdd-Screen_Shot_2022-11-29_at_5.48.47_PM.png)

### 

4\. Add the default auto reply

The reply will be automatically sent to users as private messages when users mention your Instagram business account in their Stories.

> 📘
> 
> ### 
> 
> Note
> 
> If you add more than one default reply, BotBonnie will randomly display one reply message to each user.

Click **\+ Add default reply**.

![](https://files.readme.io/6fed269-Screen_Shot_2023-05-30_at_3.56.54_PM.png)

Name the default reply and click **\+ Add reply**.

![](https://files.readme.io/6109cb9-Screen_Shot_2022-11-17_at_4.42.07_PM.png)

Each default reply can include up to 5 messages. A message can be text, image, carousel, delay, or JSON.

![](https://files.readme.io/0a56047-Screen_Shot_2022-11-17_at_5.46.47_PM.png)

Click **Confirm**. You can use the preview on the right to check the results.

The auto reply will be switched on by default and will be applied according to the operation period you set. You can use the toggle to switch off this auto reply if needed.

![](https://files.readme.io/1979206-Screen_Shot_2022-11-17_at_2.42.40_PM.png)

* * *

## 

Use case

You can encourage users to mention your brand by offering a chance to play lucky wheel or scratch-off if they mention your Instagram business account in their Stories. Users will be able to play the games and receive rewards directly in the Instagram message box.

To do this, simply add a button to the default reply, set the **On click** behavior to **Postback module** , and select a lucky wheel kit or scratch-off kit.

![](https://files.readme.io/88e2860-Screen_Shot_2023-05-30_at_4.06.24_PM.png)

For details on how to set up the lucky wheel or scratch-off kit, refer to [Lucky Wheel Kit](doc:lucky-wheel-kit) and [Scratch-Off Kit](doc:scratch-off-kit).

__Updated 6 months ago

* * *

# Automation

Use automation to handle messages across your bot based on conditions and context. When a message doesn't match [user input](doc:user-input) in your flow modules, automation determines the response:

  1. **Rule-based reply** : Conditional responses based on message content or user context.
  2. **Default reply** : Fallback response when nothing else matches.



Flow module

[ First User input ](/docs/user-input)

No match

→

⚡ Automation

[ Second Rule-based replies Match keywords and conditions ](/docs/automation-rule-based-replies)

→

[ Third Default reply Respond when nothing matches ](/docs/default-reply)

For example, the first message triggers a rule-based reply because it contains matching keywords. The second message triggers the default reply because it doesn't match any configured rules.

![](https://files.readme.io/7a549c50ee65ee97a63629842c0bb714b65f09e6d7b6e15046501c7f0ebef292-Automation-web-chat.png)

> 👍
> 
> ### 
> 
> Best practice
> 
> [Add rule-based replies](doc:automation-rule-based-replies) for common questions, then [configure a default reply](doc:automation-default-reply) to ensure your bot always responds when no rules match.

# Default Reply

The default reply is sent when no [user input](doc:user-input) or [rule-based reply](doc:automation-rule-based-replies) matches the incoming message.

> 📘
> 
> ### 
> 
> When default reply is sent
> 
> The bot sends default reply only when no user input or rule-based reply matches the message. For more information, see [How automation works](doc:automation).

* * *

## 

Set the default reply

  1. Go to **Automation** > **Auto-reply** > **Default reply** , and then toggle on **Enable default reply** or click the dropdown to begin setup.

![](https://files.readme.io/d5671cec67cd527375ff349cd1200ce19c8e7422acf4bac5f39f9c9541954494-default-reply-tab.png)

  2. Select a module from the dropdown menu to use as your fallback response. After you select a module, the module name appears below the dropdown. Click the module name to preview the message content.

![](https://files.readme.io/6a667ff14c0dc6e7c97fc3777a427831796e0e50939a52ee5c8499255f8cd12d-module-reply.png)

__Updated about 2 months ago

* * *

# Rule-based Replies

Rule-based replies let your bot respond to specific keywords in user messages. When a user's message matches a keyword, the bot responds with the dialogue module you specify, regardless of where the user is in the conversation flow.

> 📘
> 
> ### 
> 
> How rule-based replies work
> 
> The bot checks for rule-based replies if no [user input](doc:user-input) settings match the message. If no rule-based reply matches, the bot sends the [default reply](doc:automation-default-reply).

* * *

## 

Add a rule-based reply

  1. Go to **Automation** > **Auto-reply** > **Rule-based reply** , and then click **\+ Add rule-based reply**. The new rule appears at the top of the list.

![](https://files.readme.io/9173eb08f80adb96b88b978ee583803c903fa9594317a393d19c67390dc55122-rule-based-reply.png)

  2. Click the newly added rule to expand its settings. Enter the keywords that trigger the rule, and specify how the bot matches them.
     * **Contains any keywords** : The user's message can contain any of the specified keywords.
     * **Contains all keywords** : The user's message must contain all the specified keywords.
     * **Exact match** : The user's message must match the specified keyword exactly, without any additional text.

![](https://files.readme.io/361ae4758eca13635a7e5cefa63df5a88a8c271296a987831ea2ededab41bad9-edit-rule.png)

> 📘
> 
> ### 
> 
> Notes
> 
>   * Keywords aren't case-sensitive, so the capitalization doesn't affect matching.
>   * Keywords are width-sensitive, meaning full-width characters are treated as completely different symbols from standard half-width characters.
> 


  2. Under **Bot reply** , select the module from the dropdown to use as the response when the rule is triggered. After you select a module, the name appears below the list. Click the module name to preview the message content.

![](https://files.readme.io/88d74455e2257909d97269a097d4a2ce29605782bb802cebebf3cf9485cfe15f-rule-based-module.png)

  3. (Optional) Click **Set up triggered actions** to configure additional [triggered actions](doc:triggered-actions). When you are finished, click **Deploy bot** at the top right of the page to apply your changes.

![](https://files.readme.io/7fda6f69df72a26c81c43bca73b104fcfed580307cb399a489a141506e9034f4-set-up-triggered-actions.png)

* * *

## 

Managing rule-based replies

You can search for rules by name or keywords. To disable or re-enable a rule, select the toggle in the **Status** column.

![](https://files.readme.io/f8695804311fdc0416798b2765522e5f842070436e0dc0477bd75dd397ffde36-rule-based-status.png)

__Updated about 2 months ago

* * *

# Basic Information

The **Basic information** function allows you to set the following key details for your bot:

  * **Bot name**
  * **Bot cover**
  * **Language**
  * **Bot purpose**
  * **Bot object**
  * **Blackout window**



These settings help ensure your bot operates effectively and aligns with your marketing and engagement goals.

To configure your bot's information, go to **Settings** , then click the **Basic information** tab.

![](https://files.readme.io/b1a335171215674c367d6e9ac1602b86dfb79502b5c40926b569a96bad1cbc0e-basic-information.png)

> 📘
> 
> ### 
> 
> Note
> 
> Click **Save** after making any changes to apply your updates.

* * *

## 

Bot name

Enter a name for your bot to help identify it in the chatbot console.

* * *

## 

Bot cover

Upload a JPG or PNG image to represent your bot visually. This image may be used as the bot’s avatar in interfaces and communications.

![](https://files.readme.io/f7176a3a20bc6d46374b88346bdd4be7fdadd4798bb57af02fd93d164b7aebf2-bot-cover.png)

* * *

## 

Time zone

The time zone displayed in the chatbot console is automatically assigned based on the region of the connected social media service. This setting cannot be adjusted within the console.

The selected time zone directly impacts campaign scheduling across various chatbot features, determining when activities are triggered:  
  
---  
Flow > Advanced kits   
• [Survey kit](doc:survey-kit)   
• [Receipt registration kit](doc:receipt-registration-kit)   
• [Lucky wheel kit](doc:lucky-wheel-kit)   
• Scratch-off kit   
• [MGM kit](doc:mgm-kit)   
• [Daily check-in kit](doc:daily-checkin-kit)   
• [Prize distribution kit](doc:prize-distribution-kit)   
• [Point card kit](doc:point-card-kit)  
Content > [Menus](doc:menus)  
[Growth tools](doc:start-chats)  
[Broadcasts](doc:broadcasting-create- broadcasts)  
  
If you need to modify the time zone, please contact your customer success manager for assistance.

* * *

## 

Language

The selected language applies to the chatbot console interface and does not affect the language displayed to end users.

* * *

## 

Bot purpose

Select the purpose of your bot:

  * Practical commercial use: Intended for business operations and user engagement.
  * Testing and demonstration: For internal testing, technical development, or showcasing chatbot functionality.



* * *

## 

Bot object

Specify whether the chatbot is built for your own Facebook Pages or LINE Official Accounts, or for a client’s use case to categorize bot ownership and management.

* * *

## 

Blackout window

The Blackout window pauses **[Sequences](doc:sequence-kit)** during specified time periods. Messages scheduled to send during a blackout period will be delayed until the blackout period ends, ensuring better message timing and user experience.

> 📘
> 
> ### 
> 
> Note
> 
> The Blackout window feature may be subject to different regulations in various countries.

Click **\+ Time slot** to add a blackout period. Select specific days or a recurring range (such as, "Monday to Friday") and set the time range for when sequences should be paused.

![](https://files.readme.io/7df4f0cb71143b2d62910436dc6e2c79ed273a24be691cdc844bc29ef18c359e-blackout-window.png)

__Updated 6 months ago

* * *

[Status Check](/docs/status-check)[Business Hours](/docs/business-hours)

# Blind Box

> 👍
> 
> ### 
> 
> Supported channels
> 
>   * Messenger
>   * Instagram
>   * LINE
>   * WebChat
>   * KakaoTalk
> 


Use the blind box kit to create a campaign that allows users to play a mystery unboxing game from their mobile device or web browser. With the blind box kit, you can easily:

  * Customize the game appearance and unboxing experience.
  * Manage prize inventory and distribution probabilities.
  * View detailed performance data in the kit report page.

![](https://files.readme.io/c231e69ceef2066f23abea88d3214ecc62240595061f4a4a807288b218f2c95a-blind-box-mobile.png)

* * *

## 

1\. Add the kit to your flow

From the left menu, go to **Flow**. Select **Advanced kits** , and then select **Blind box**. To configure the settings, click **Start editing** or double-click the kit module.

![](https://files.readme.io/36fee2be9e032b12115257d56de68856d4969d90540ae7f178a59e8040a82fac-advanced-kit-blind-box.png)

* * *

## 

2\. Configure basic settings

Enter a kit name to identify the kit in your flow, set campaign period by selecting a start and end time, and define the entry limits.

![](https://files.readme.io/8e95a9fc50fe6d47869e1a49f3ce4c6d3ac0494513b65092e250b990636d5ad1-blind-box-basic-settings.png)

### 

Number of entries

Set the maximum number of times a user can play the blind box game.

  * **Maximum entries per user** : Specify the total number of times a user can play the game during the campaign period.
  * **Maximum entries per day** : Specify how many times a user can play the game each day. The count resets at midnight daily.
  * **Use a parameter value as the number of entries** : Dynamically apply entry limits for each user based on an associated parameter value.



* * *

## 

3\. Design the game appearance

In the **Prize and Appearance** section, configure the visual elements of the main display, which is the first screen users see when entering the game with following elements:

  * **Game name** : The title displayed at the top of the card.
  * **Game box** : Adjust the primary and secondary colors and modify the button text for starting the game.
  * **Theme color** : The primary color of the game interface.
  * **Background image** : Upload a branded graphic to serve as the wallpaper for the game on the user's device. Ensure your image file meets the following specifications:
    * Recommended image size: 480 × 720 pixels
    * Recommended aspect ratio: Close to 2:3
    * Maximum file size: 500 KB

![](https://files.readme.io/c263c8ecf6e535e9d9d8f8fec0716937b5540ac7dd73aafe4af9b02045aa83a6-blind-box-main-display.png)

* * *

## 

4\. Configure the prizes

Define the prizes to award, including their inventory, probability, and format.

  * Add and manage prizes
  * Winning and non-winning probability
  * Configure the prize stock quantity
  * Supported prize formats
  * Prize distribution logic
  * Customize winning and losing screens



### 

Add and manage prizes

Each blind box game requires at least two prizes. To add another prize, click **\+ Add prize**.

> 👍
> 
> ### 
> 
> Best practice
> 
> We recommend adding a maximum of 20 prizes to ensure the **Kit report** diagram displays clearly. A larger list may make it challenging to read.

Click and expand the section to configure the following prize settings:

Field| Description  
---|---  
**Name**|  The prize title users see when they win.  
**Probability**|  The chance percentage of winning this specific prize. For more details of prize probabilities, see Winning and non-winning probability.  
**Format**|  The type of prize. For details on configuration options, see Supported prize formats.  
**Image**|  The image of the prize shown on the prizes list and on the winning screen.  
**Details**|  Extra text for the prize.  
**Quantity**|  Set the available inventory of the prize. This setting is unavailable for prizes with the prize format set as **Unique serial number**.  
**Triggered actions**|  Configure automated events when a prize is won. Select **Send a message through chat** to notify users when they win or add a [triggered action](doc:triggered-actions).  
**Redemption period**|  Set the redemption timeframe for the prize to a specific period, match the campaign period, or no expiry.  
**Distribution settings**|  Control the pacing of stock for each prize.   
  
• **Evenly distribute across campaign** : The system automatically calculates a daily limit to ensure stock lasts until the campaign end date. To learn more about how this setting distributes your prize stock, see Evenly distributing prizes.   
  
• **Limit daily distribution** : Set a manual cap on how many prizes can be won per day, for example, distribute up to 10 prizes per day. When a prize is running out, the other prize(s) winning probability will not be changed.   
  
• **Distribute until depleted** : Prizes are awarded on a first-come, first-served basis until the **Quantity** reaches zero. When a prize is running out, the other prize(s) winning probability will not be changed.  
  
### 

Winning and non-winning probability

As you configure the probabilities for each prize above, the **Win probability** (sum of all prizes) and **Non-winning probability** (chance of losing) are automatically calculated at the bottom of the list.

You can optionally check the **Generate prize for every participant** box to ensure a 100% winning probabilities for every user to win a prize. If you leave this box unchecked, individual prize probabilities apply and the winning probability is not guaranteed.

![](https://files.readme.io/596dc66f7f0db70d86f7364e0c59272a67ab37183b412975a1b61cdafaea4607-blind-box-prize-distribution.png)

### 

Configure the prize stock quantity

Use the **Prize quantity display** setting to control whether prize stock numbers are visible on the prize card:

  * **Do not show** : Users see no inventory numbers.
  * **Show total quantity** : Users see the initial total stock.
  * **Show remaining quantity** : Users see how many prizes are left in real-time.



### 

Supported prize formats

Select the format that matches your reward type.

  * **Inform users of prizes won**
  * **Physical exchange**
  * **Unique serial number**
  * **Fixed serial number**
  * **Point card**



#### 

Inform users of prizes won

Users are immediately notified of their winning status. This prize type is ideal for digital prizes that don't require a physical exchange to occur. For example, this prize type can be used for digital assets such as images.

![](https://files.readme.io/0894be5-Screen_Shot_2022-10-03_at_11.52.57_AM.png)

#### 

Physical exchange

This prize type is suitable for brick-and-mortar locations and physical events, where winning users can physically redeem a prize by presenting the winning screen to an employee.

![](https://files.readme.io/a1d9b32-Screen_Shot_2022-10-03_at_12.02.12_PM.png)

#### 

Unique serial number

Upload a CSV file containing a list of unique serial numbers (text, barcode, or URL) that users can exchange for a prize. If this prize type is selected, winners will see the serial number when they win. For example:

  * **Text** : Users who win this prize will be assigned a unique serial number from the CSV file in sequential order. Serial numbers are suitable for coupons in e-commerce shops or rewards in mobile games.
  * **Barcode** : The serial number will be converted and displayed as a barcode. Barcodes are suitable for physical stores with POS machines.
  * **URL** : The CSV file should contain a list of URLs. When users click the button, they will be directed to the URL to receive the reward. URLs are suitable for distributing LINE points, as it allows users to redeem points with a single click.

![](https://files.readme.io/08193c1-Screen_Shot_2022-10-03_at_1.59.22_PM.png)

#### 

Fixed serial number

Specify a serial number (text, barcode, or URL) that users can exchange for a prize. If this prize type is selected, winners will see the serial number when they win. For example:

  * **Text** : Users who win this prize will be shown the serial number. Serial number are suitable for coupons in e-commerce shops or rewards in mobile games.
  * **Barcode** : The serial number will be converted and displayed as a barcode. Barcodes are suitable for physical stores with POS machines.
  * **URL** : When users click the button, they will be directed to the URL to receive the reward. URLs are suitable for distributing LINE points, as it allows users to redeem points with a single click.



#### 

Point card

Select an existing [point card kit](doc:point-card-kit) to send to users who win.

### 

Prize distribution logic

When setting distribution probabilities for prizes, please note the system behaviors regarding non-winning probability, how winning chances adjust when a prize runs out, and the specific calculation applied when the Evenly distribute across campaign setting is selected.

#### 

Non-winning probability

If the configured prize probabilities don't add up to 100%, the remainder represents the **Non-winning probability** , which is the chance that a user does not win any prize.

For example, given two prizes, "Prize A" and "Prize B", each with a probability of 30%, totaling 60% (30% + 30% = 60%), the Non-winning probability would be 40% (100% - 60% = 40%).

#### 

When a prize runs out

If there are multiple prizes and one prize runs out, the probability of winning the remaining prize(s) stays the same, while the non-winning probability increases.

Prize| Original probability| Probability after Prize A runs out  
---|---|---  
Prize A| 10%| 0% (Runs out)  
Prize B| 20%| 20% (Unchanged)  
Prize C| 30%| 30% (Unchanged)  
No prize| 40%| 50%  
  
#### 

Logic for evenly distributing prizes

When **Evenly distribute across campaign** is selected, the system automatically recalculates the distribution daily based on the remaining prizes and days. The maximum number of prizes to be distributed by the end of a given day is calculated as follows:

  * `Campaign elapsed time (days) / Total campaign duration (days) * Prize pool quantity`



The difference between the total expected number of distributed prizes and the actual number of distributed prizes determines the limit for that day.

> 📘
> 
> ### 
> 
> Note
> 
> If the daily distribution maximum isn't a whole number, it will be rounded up to the nearest whole number. This can result in variations in the daily prize distribution limit for each day of the campaign.

For example, given a campaign period of seven days, and a single prize, "Prize A", with a quantity of 30 and a 100% probability of winning (no other prizes, no chance of losing), the estimated counts for the daily distribution are listed in the table below:

Day| Daily calculation| Prizes distributed so far| Prizes to distribute today  
---|---|---|---  
1| `(1/7 * 30) = 4.28 → 5`  
  
A maximum of 5 prizes should be distributed by the end of day one.| 0 prizes| `(5 - 0) = 5`  
  
Up to 5 prizes can be distributed today.  
2| `(2/7 * 30) = 8.57 → 9`  
  
A maximum of 9 prizes should be distributed by the end of day two.| 5 prizes| `(9 - 5) = 4`  
  
Up to 4 prizes can be distributed today.  
3| `(3/7 * 30) = 12.85 → 13`  
  
A maximum of 13 prizes should be distributed by the end of day three.| 9 prizes| `(13 - 9) = 4`  
  
Up to 4 prizes can be distributed today  
4| `(4/7 * 30) = 17.14 → 18`  
  
A maximum of 18 prizes should be distributed by the end of day four.| 13 prizes| `(18 - 5) = 5`  
  
Up to 5 prizes can be distributed today.  
5| `(5/7 * 30) = 21.42 → 22`  
  
A maximum of 22 prizes should be distributed by the end of day five.| 18 prizes| `(22 - 18) = 4`  
  
Up to 4 prizes can be distributed today.  
6| `(6/7 * 30) = 25.71 → 26`  
  
A maximum of 26 prizes should be distributed by the end of day six.| 22 prizes| `(26 - 22) = 4`  
  
Up to 4 prizes can be distributed today.  
7| `(7/7 * 30) = 30`  
  
A maximum of 30 prizes should be distributed by the end of day seven.| 26 prizes| `(30 - 26) = 4`  
  
Up to 4 prizes can be distributed today.  
  
### 

Customize winning and losing screens

Use the panel on the right to preview the screen that users see based on their game results.

  * **Winning screen** : Expand this section to modify the text on the card to congratulate winners. To customize the accompanying chat notification, click the specific prize modules in the sub-flow.
  * **Losing screen** : Select the **Send notification in chat** checkbox to send a message in addition to displaying the result card. To customize this notification, click the "Try next time" module in the sub-flow.

![](https://files.readme.io/69e59d1dbbc375393997e7a18f29b578b7e53af531816c9d6026322bb6871732-blind-box-win-lose-screen.png)

* * *

## 

5\. Add terms and conditions

The kit provides a default terms and conditions statement listing the rules for participation in this campaign. These terms and conditions are displayed at the bottom of the game card screen. You can modify this field to customize the terms and conditions statement to suit your campaign.

* * *

## 

Blind box report

To view your blind box kit performance, go to **Analytics** > **Kit Report**. This report displays key metrics including total participants, total number of entries, and prize status.

![](https://files.readme.io/7d8d0629b8cdfa04571a4e2b7709376ee80ac79f73c87ad42cc4afd123fc92ea-kit-report-blind-box.png)

You can also export a CSV containing a list of participants and their associated data:

  1. Click **Export participant list**.
  2. In the modal that pops up, enter the email address where you want to receive the CSV file, and click **Confirm**.

![](https://files.readme.io/9d426ebe10bd123ebce05fc2c805328d61530cb78cacb81db3e7516bffa7d3d3-blind-box-report-export-csv.png)

An email containing the file will be sent within one hour. The participant list contains the following details:

  * User ID
  * User name
  * Conversation platform
  * User mobile
  * User email
  * User birthday
  * User location
  * User gender
  * Prize title
  * Prize format
  * Serial number
  * Kit ID
  * Drawn time
  * Redeemed time
  * User profile picture



* * *

## 

Reset a kit

Reset a kit to refresh all data associated with the kit:

  * All records of participant data (like user details, draws, and wins) will be deleted.
  * All prizes will be returned to the prize pool.
  * All data in the kit report will be cleared.



> 🚧
> 
> ### 
> 
> Caution
> 
> Resetting the kit can't be reversed. Once the kit is refreshed, all records of participant data and prize distribution (including the kit report) will be cleared and all prizes will be returned to the prize pool.

In the blind box kit settings, go to the **Reset** section, then click **Reset**. You'll be prompted to enter "Reset" into the input box, then click **Reset** again to confirm your choice.

![](https://files.readme.io/745f2a0e1bac235d0111c74d2d82a0bff14f4425a35a1d709402ce178b1ecc4f-reset-kit.png)

__Updated 10 days ago

* * *

# Broadcast Reports

Broadcast reports become available a few minutes after you send a campaign. To view performance data, go to the **Sent broadcasts** list and click the view icon next to a broadcast campaign.

![](https://files.readme.io/635ef9c93019f3580ab7619473b2fdd05604e78da49dba0a79861d3c520c82a0-view-data.png)

* * *

## 

Performance overview

The **Performance overview** section shows your broadcast's performance metrics:

  * Total sent, delivered, read, and clicked users
  * Total conversions and conversion value

![](https://files.readme.io/8e6b88cf3e54b9bcacd01c408af0daaa438ba73f7d235676939b49b09e211540-performance-overview.png)

### 

Export detailed report

> 📘
> 
> ### 
> 
> Supported channel
> 
>   * [LINE: Mass broadcasts and narrowcasts](doc:broadcasting-create-broadcasts#line)
> 


To download a CSV file of the report, click the **Export detailed report** button in the top-right corner. Enter your email address to receive the file, and then click **Confirm**.

![](https://files.readme.io/de5c12172491e14acc0ee83185503fcefc293b3aa360e1428fa56d726a33a7dd-export-broadcast-report.png) ![](https://files.readme.io/25d9bb1-Screen_Shot_2023-08-31_at_8.46.31_AM.png)

### 

Key metrics

The following table shows key metrics for supported broadcast types:

Metric| Description| LINE (Mass broadcast and narrowcast)| LINE (Targeted broadcast)| Messenger| KakaoTalk  
---|---|---|---|---|---  
Sent users| Total estimated users who were sent broadcast.| ✓| ✓| ✓| ✓  
Delivered users| Users who successfully received the message.| ✓| ✓| ✓| ✓  
Read users| Users who opened and viewed the message.| ✓| N/A| ✓| N/A  
Clicked users| Users who interacted with buttons or quick replies in the broadcast.| ✓| ✓| ✓| ✓  
Conversions| The total number of conversion made within 24 hours of a user's last click on the broadcast.| ✓| ✓| N/A| N/A  
Conv. value (Conversion value)| The total revenue from all conversion counted in the conversions metric.| ✓| ✓| N/A| N/A  
CVR (Conversion rate)| The percentage of users who successfully completed the conversion after clicking the broadcast. Calculated using: (Conversions / clicked users) x 100%.| ✓| ✓| N/A| N/A  
CTR (Click-through rate)| The percentage of sent broadcast that resulted in a click. Calculated using: (Clicked users / sent users) × 100%| ✓| ✓| N/A| N/A  
  
> 📘
> 
> ### 
> 
> Note
> 
> For mass broadcasts and narrowcasts, the **Read users** and **Clicked users** metrics are provided directly by the LINE API. However, LINE will not return this data if the audience size is below a certain threshold.

#### 

Conversion tracking requirements

Conversion data (conversions and conversion value) requires Appier SDK installation and conversion event configuration. To get started, contact your customer success manager.

After integrating the Appier SDK and configuring `product_purchased` as the conversion event, conversion data uses the [**Last-click attribution model**](https://docs.aiqua.appier.com/docs/performance-and-reports-understanding-event-attribution#last-click-model) with a 24-hour attribution window by default.

Each user can generate multiple conversions from one broadcast, so the conversion rate (CVR) may exceed 100% when users make repeat purchases from the same broadcast.

* * *

## 

Funnel

The **Funnel** chart visualizes your campaign's performance as a conversion funnel, helping you quickly identify where users drop off in the engagement process. The funnel displays conversion percentages for each stage and calculates churn rates between stages.

![](https://files.readme.io/ff3f2edc0b07d2d2610f0892a1c8e49b15f92b6b179ee87f735ca0ba7141d746-conversion-funnel.png)

### 

Button click analytics

You can also click **View total button clicks** to see a breakdown of clicks for each interactive element in your broadcast. This option is available for LINE targeted broadcasts, Messenger, and KakaoTalk broadcasts. You can track click performance for the following broadcast types and actions:

Broadcast type| Action type| Supported message types  
---|---|---  
Mass broadcast| Open URL| • Text  
• Carousel  
• Imagemap  
Mass broadcast| Postback| N/A  
Narrowcast| Open URL| • Text  
• Carousel  
• Imagemap  
Narrowcast| Postback| N/A  
Targeted broadcast| Open URL| • Text  
• Carousel  
• Imagemap  
Targeted broadcast| Postback| • Text  
• Carousel  
• Imagemap  
• Quick reply button  
  
![](https://files.readme.io/c0bf638a58d888fa46acef25c709b35112680e11acda8ab5d5c0af4c8401657c-button-clicks.png)

* * *

## 

Performance details

In the **Performance details** section, you can:

  * Filter and view the broadcast performance by channel.
  * Adjust date range to focus on a specific period.

![](https://files.readme.io/c23dfcec51a3d0640e057685dc56284e0004e9609c9ea9174743b4d290529471-performance-details.png)

* * *

## 

Performance trend

The **Performance trend** section displays how your selected metrics perform over time within your chosen date range. Use the dropdown to select a performance metric to analyze, and view the timeline visualization as a line chart.

![](https://files.readme.io/2e462ddd7dc6c500b666d07be4126f3b7267f70a642cc84172780fe6d26ce9ab-performance-trend.png)

__Updated 2 months ago
