# Create Broadcasts

You can send broadcast messages to your users through Messenger, LINE Official Account, and KakaoTalk. Using BotBonnie, you can target the right audience based on different conditions, such as tags, last interaction time, and account linking status.

![](https://files.readme.io/47ff1a0-Screen_Shot_2023-09-05_at_10.34.28_AM.png)

BotBonnie also offers an **AI Click Optimization** feature that predicts users who are more likely to click on your broadcasts, allowing you to target the right users and increase click-through rate (CTR). By excluding users who are not likely to click, you can potentially save broadcast costs and avoid disturbing users who are not interested.

![](https://files.readme.io/5f1049d-Screen_Shot_2023-09-05_at_10.33.12_AM.png)

> 📘
> 
> ### 
> 
> AI Click Optimization
> 
>   * AI Click Optimization is a premium feature. Contact your customer success manager for more details.
>   * You will need to have past broadcast data in order to use this feature. The AI model analyzes user behaviors based on the performance of recent broadcasts that include a clickable URL.
>   * For more details on the requirements and the setup steps, see Set up AI Click Optimization.
> 


Below are the different ways to filter broadcast audience and the AI features supported by each channel.

Features| LINE OA| Messenger| KakaoTalk  
---|---|---|---  
Ways to filter audience| • Filter by condition   
• Filter by segment| Filter by condition| Filter by condition  
AI Click Optimization| Supported| N/A| N/A  
  
* * *

## 

1\. Create a new broadcast

Go to **Broadcasting > Broadcasts**, then click **\+ Create broadcast**.

![](https://files.readme.io/a045f692e76bcc2682d82b1a48f0a6f5ee5085ca5ba6fb84374c4670b81919c7-broadcast-create.png)

* * *

## 

2\. Select the broadcast channel

Select the channels you want to broadcast to.

You can select multiple Messenger, LINE Official Accounts (LINE OA), or KakaoTalk channels, but the channels selected need to be of the same messaging platform.

![](https://files.readme.io/d3777ac927099ce1091d21e13cea55a4de1fac60df826d89da080f8e3a63c2a4-select-channel.png)

> 📘
> 
> ### 
> 
> Expected receivers
> 
>   * Messenger: If you see a reload symbol 🔄 and the expected receiver count is not displayed, make sure you have admin access to the Facebook Page.
>   * KakaoTalk: Only users with a saved phone number are included in the expected receiver count, as broadcasts are sent via phone number. This number may not match the user count in the user list.
>   * Viber: Only users with a [saved phone number](doc:channels-viber#phone-number-requirements) can be included in the broadcast audience.
> 


* * *

## 

3\. Filter the broadcast audience

See the available filtering methods and message types for each platform below:

Filter method| LINE| Messenger| KakaoTalk| Viber  
---|---|---|---|---  
Filter by condition:  
no condition set| • Targeted broadcast  
• Mass broadcast| All message types| All message types| All message types  
Filter by condition:  
conditions set| Targeted broadcast| All message types| All message types| All message types  
Filter by segment| Narrowcast| N/A| N/A| N/A  
  
> 📘
> 
> ### 
> 
> LINE broadcasts
> 
> A filter method will directly impacts the performance metrics you can track. See the [Broadcast reports](doc:broadcasting-broadcast-reports) section for full details.

### 

Filter by condition

You can set up one or multiple [filter conditions](doc:user-list#available-filter-conditions), or no conditions at all.

  * If you do not select a filter condition, the broadcast message will be sent to all users who have interacted with the channel.
  * If you want to set multiple filter conditions, click **Add condition**. When using multiple conditions, choose how they work together:
    * **Match all** : Target users who meet all rules you set.
    * **Match any** : Target users who meet at least one rule you set.

![](https://files.readme.io/4429c930d36ad858553618505f915ccb9e55b2586f23af86585c91ec54f7c797-add-condition-broadcast.gif)

### 

Filter by segment

You can select an existing segment. To create a segment, go to **Audience** > **[Segments](doc:segments)**.

![](https://files.readme.io/d686e2421e80e571d6ca0046672f6e4fcf7531f81eb2148c47e13e2736210453-filter-by-segment.png)

* * *

## 

4\. Set broadcast time

Set a broadcast time and click **Next**.

  * **Broadcast now** : The broadcast will be sent after you complete all the broadcast settings. Note that it may take up to a few minutes for users to receive the broadcast depending on the size of your receivers.
  * **Schedule broadcast** : You can set a broadcast time that is at least 30 minutes and up to six months from now.

![](https://files.readme.io/e674e35aee4bded91b68d7e7280775b8112cdd1a66afa57b2c4903564b4aafc0-broadcast-time.png)

> 📘
> 
> ### 
> 
> Note
> 
> Avoid modifying the broadcast content within 30 minutes of the scheduled time.

* * *

## 

5\. Select a message tag

Select a message tag is required if you are broadcasting through LINE or Messenger.

For creating KakaoTalk broadcasts, you can skip this step and proceed directly to Step 6. Set up the broadcast module.

### 

LINE

If you are broadcasting through LINE Official Account, select one of the following messaging types based on how you filter the target audience.

  * **Targeted broadcast** : This option is only supported if you have selected **Filter by condition**. The broadcast will be sent to users who meet the filter conditions selected and have interacted with the chatbot before.
  * **Mass broadcast** : This option is only supported if you did not set any filter conditions. The broadcast will be sent to all users who are friends with your LINE Official Account, including users who have not interacted with you before. Those who blocked your LINE Official Account are excluded.
  * **Narrowcast** : This option is only supported if you have selected **Filter by segment**. The broadcast will be sent to users in the segment who have interacted with the chatbot before.

![](https://files.readme.io/923b46be0ef234d78d71e57950e3eecf8599958c0e9c8ba433f108f22431f1f6-message-tag.png)

### 

Messenger

If you are broadcasting through Messenger, select one of the following messaging tags based on the content and purpose of your broadcast.

  * **Confirmed event update** : Send users confirmations, reminders or updates for an event they have registered for, such as purchased tickets. This tag can be used for upcoming events and events in progress. Promotional content is not allowed.
  * **Post-purchase update** : Send users updates on recent purchases, such as transaction details and shipment status notifications. Promotional content is not allowed.
  * **Account update** : Send users notifications about their application or account, such as credit card application or suspicious activity notifications. Promotional content is not allowed.
  * **Non-promotional subscription update** : Subscription messaging is only available for news organizations that successfully register their Pages with Meta's [News Page Index (NPI)](https://www.facebook.com/business/help/377680816096171?id=644465919618833). Promotional content is not allowed.
  * **Standard messages** : The content can be promotional. The broadcast will only be sent to users who have interacted with your Messenger within the last 24 hours. See [Messenger's policy](https://developers.facebook.com/docs/messenger-platform/policy/policy-overview#standard_messaging) on what counts as an interaction.

![](https://files.readme.io/87762d337bbeca9029becd93515663d2117ab93f1019cd51b62329f4cbdc211a-message-tag-fb.png)

> 🚧
> 
> ### 
> 
> Important
> 
> Carefully choose your message type based on the content of your broadcast. Meta may suspend you if it finds that you have abused them.
> 
>   * Promotional content can only be sent through **Standard messages**.
>   * See [Meta's guide](https://developers.facebook.com/docs/messenger-platform/reference/send-api#message-tag-usages) on the allowed usages of different message tags.
> 


* * *

## 

6\. Set up the broadcast module

Select an existing broadcast module or click **Add module** to create a new one. The available message types will differ depend on the messaging platform you're broadcasting on.

![](https://files.readme.io/44823e3b7a4a5c214d663effaeac9c785affdce6f319f96f921a941683d4772d-set-up-module.png)

Channel| Supported message types  
---|---  
LINE| • Text   
• Image   
• Carousel   
• Image carousel   
• Video   
• Audio   
• Imagemap   
• Video imagemap   
• One-time notification request   
• JSON  
Messenger| • Text   
• Image   
• Carousel   
• Video   
• Audio   
• Delay   
• One-time notification request   
• Recurring notifications request   
• JSON  
KakaoTalk| • Basic text   
• Wide image   
• Wide list   
• Carousel commerce   
• Carousel feed  
Viber| • Text (Viber)   
• Image (Viber)  
  
> 📘
> 
> ### 
> 
> Kakao Moment: Advertising message
> 
> This option must be enabled if the broadcast message contains any promotional content (for example, special discounts, promotions, or product-related events) even if the message includes both promotional and non-promotional content. Enabling this option is legally required in some countries.
> 
> ![](https://files.readme.io/5ba7b0e-Screen_Shot_2024-01-12_at_4.21.15_PM.png)

* * *

## 

7\. (Optional) Set up AI Click Optimization

> 📘
> 
> ### 
> 
> Requirements
> 
>   * AI Click Optimization is a premium feature. Contact your customer success manager for more details.
>   * To use AI Click Optimization, the following requirements must be met:
>     * AI Click Optimization can only be used on a single LINE Official Account.
>     * The message tag must be **Targeted broadcast** or **Narrowcast**.
>     * The broadcast module must include an URL.
>     * The broadcast needs to be scheduled at least one hour from now.
>     * The expected receivers need to exceed 1000 users.
> 


To use this feature, click **Start prediction**. It'll take a few minutes for the prediction to complete.

![](https://files.readme.io/aea010080cf0bf59e3285796f848bfb5048c5fe67b75e1e663d748edc88802b8-start-prediction.png)

After the prediction is completed, you will see a slide bar that indicates the number of estimated receivers and the estimated CTR. The **Optimal** point is the sweet spot calculated by AI that boosts CTR but still includes enough users, striking a balance between precision and coverage.

You can drag the slide bar or enter a user size to adjust the number of receivers to include. If you move the slide bar to the right, more users will be included, but the estimated CTR will also drop because the target audience now includes more users who are less likely to click. The opposite is true if you move the slide bar to the left.

![](https://files.readme.io/501ef8b2153b95bb59fa46151bc697a2975f6a071262f19ad36c83b6726dad25-ai-prediction-broadcast.gif)

> 📘
> 
> ### 
> 
> Note
> 
> The **Estimated CTR** is based on the prediction generated by the AI model. The actual CTR may differ from the prediction. The message content you used will also affect the actual CTR.

* * *

## 

8\. Review and complete the settings

Click **Next**. Review the broadcast settings and click **Schedule** or **Send**.

![](https://files.readme.io/bc1ecc8ddff8707577a4a96605c0d5d06670f8a6d1b1bfd974a9f09511280568-send-broadcast.png)

You'll find the broadcast under either **Scheduled broadcasts** or **Sent broadcasts**.

![](https://files.readme.io/0e9776452fcdd325c3992578726758a156c6a2e4909b91438d8d2d1ab3973705-broadcast-list.png)

__Updated 4 months ago

* * *

# Business Hours

In **Settings** , go to the **Business hours** tab to configure the following settings:

  * Business hours for your WebChat widget.
  * Bot pause settings for live chat.

![](https://files.readme.io/09902ce3f668e6bf0ee2395672a29e17f58c5ca2abd9173873b3e4a408ee2a93-settings-business-hours.png)

* * *

## 

Business hours

Use **Business hours** to manage customer service agent availability and customer interactions based on defined working hours.

During business hours, an **Online** icon will be displayed in the WebChat. Outside of business hours, an offline icon will be displayed, helping users understand agent availability.

### 

Configure your WebChat business hours

Follow these steps to configure your business hours:

  1. Toggle the **Business hours** switch to enable the feature.

![](https://files.readme.io/9ce85b1e8edf5d8369c7a9761cf5c0e1d3a02936e073b3bd461625a4c31c6a5d-business-hours-on.png)

  2. Select the days of operation, then choose a start and end time from the dropdown menus.

![](https://files.readme.io/a0cdafbe4ff989c60bbcc3bb2909e5b28b3f5c4bbe7ccd1485d361bedc9f50f2-business-hours-dropdown.png)

  3. Click **\+ Add business hours** to configure multiple time slots if needed.

![](https://files.readme.io/36f0ac737e0c49fc1db79b2cd63c4c2d98b36164b039f509a71144be27adde97-business-hours-add-slot.png)

> 📘
> 
> ### 
> 
> Note
> 
> The default timezone is Asia/Taipei. If you have any concerns, contact your customer success manager.

After configuring business hours, you may want to customize settings in the **[Notify agent](doc:settings-notify-agent)** tab to optimize how your chatbot transfers conversations to human agents and handles auto-reply outside business hours.

* * *

## 

Pause bot in live chat

Configure how long to pause the chatbot's automated responses when a live agent manually takes over a conversation. The bot pauses in the following scenarios:

  * A live agent sends a message in the chatroom.
  * A chat is moved to the **Main** category.



The bot automatically resumes when the chat is moved to the **Done** category.

### 

Enable and configure pause duration

Follow these steps to configure the bot pause duration:

  1. Toggle the **Pause bot (Live chat)** switch to enable this feature.
  2. Specify the number of minutes the bot should remain paused.

![](https://files.readme.io/67ac527ba8dfe1c0859c1445c8a539dab14665108fb7206de6d67141c58db09f-pausebot-livechat.png)

Changes take effect immediately after saving.

#### 

Feature comparison

To better understand the differences between **Pause bot (Live chat)** and **[Pause bot (Assign to live agent)](doc:settings-notify-agent#pause-bot-assign-to-live-agent)** , here’s a comparison table that highlights the key distinctions:

Feature| Pause bot (Live chat)| Pause bot (Assign to live agent)  
---|---|---  
Purpose| Pauses the bot when a live agent manually takes over a conversation.| Pauses the bot when a user triggers the **Assign to live agent** action.  
Trigger| A live agent sends a message or moves the chat to the **Main** category.| The **[Assign to live agent](doc:triggered-actions#assign-to-live-agent)** triggered action is activated.  
Resumes| Resumes when the chat is moved to the **Done** category.| Resumes at the end of the ongoing conversation, business hours, or after a specified duration.  
  
__Updated 7 days ago

# Buttons

Certain message types can contain up to three buttons that initiate the following functions when clicked:

  * **Postback module** : Send a module in the chat.
  * **Open URL** : Open a URL with optional UTM and query parameters
  * **Open kit directly:** Take the user to a page inside an advanced kit directly, such as the "Start game" page of a lucky wheel kit. Supported kit types are lucky wheel, scratch-off, daily check-in, point card, prize distribution, and MGM.
  * **Make phone call** : Call a phone number.
  * **Share on Facebook** : Share a message on the user's Facebook wall. See [Share on Facebook](https://docs.botbonnie.appier.com/docs/share-on-facebook).
  * **Share with LINE friend** : Share a message with a LINE friend. See [Share with LINE friend](https://docs.botbonnie.appier.com/docs/share-with-line-friend).
  * **Open live agent chatroom** : Open a [live agent chatroom](doc:live-chat#live-agent-chatroom-line) in a chat conversation. Only supported for LINE.
  * **Integrated EC service** : Access features related to [e-commerce integrations](doc:e-commerce-integrations), such as account linking and viewing order history

![](https://files.readme.io/660f89d-buttons.png)

The following message types can contain buttons:

  * [Text](doc:message-type-text)
  * [Carousel](doc:message-type-carousel)
  * [Card](doc:message-type-card)
  * [Image carousel](doc:message-type-image-carousel)
  * [Video imagemap](doc:message-type-video-imagemap)
  * [One-time notification request](doc:message-type-one-time-notification-request)
  * [Recurring notifications request](doc:message-type-recurring-notifications-request)



* * *

## 

Add a button

To add a button to a message:

  1. Click **\+ Add button** in the message where you'd like to add the button. A button settings window will appear.

![](https://files.readme.io/a4950cd-add-button.png)

  2. Under **Button name** , input the button label. This will be visible to the user.
     * Button names are limited to 20 characters or less.
     * Button names for the following message types are fixed and can't be modified: [one-time notification request](doc:message-type-one-time-notification-request), [recurring notifications request](doc:message-type-recurring-notifications-request).

![](https://files.readme.io/553fe25-Screen_Shot_2024-01-04_at_1.11.02_PM.png)

  3. Under **On click** , click the dropdown menu and select the action the button will initiate when clicked.
     * The On click setting for buttons in [video imagemap](doc:message-type-video-imagemap) messages is fixed to **Open URL**. This setting can't be changed.

![](https://files.readme.io/c1db5dc-Screen_Shot_2024-01-04_at_1.11.02_PM.png)

  4. (Optional) Click **Set up triggered actions** to add triggered actions. For configuration details, see [Triggered Actions](doc:triggered-actions#triggered-action-settings). Adding a triggered action is only supported for the following button **On click** options:

     1. **Postback module**
     2. **Open URL**
     3. **Share on Facebook**
     4. **Share with LINE Friend**
     5. **Open live agent chatroom**

![](https://files.readme.io/06b6772-button-triggered-actions.png)

  4. Click **Done** and deploy your changes.



* * *

## 

Reorder buttons

To change the horizontal order in which the buttons are displayed in your message:

  1. Hover over the button you'd like to reorder.
  2. Click on the reposition icon that appears on the left side.
  3. Drag and drop the button to the desired position.

![](https://files.readme.io/c1c78d7-reorder-button.png)

* * *

## 

Delete a button

To delete a button, open the button's settings, then click **Delete** in the bottom left corner of the settings window.

# Getting Started with Channel Integrations

Integrate BotBonnie with your messaging channels allows you to better engage your users with less effort. Using BotBonnie, you can:

  * Automate customer services with chatbots and [AI rules](doc:ai-rules) or auto-replies, and seamlessly hand off to live agents when needed.
  * Engage users with prebuilt templates and gamification kits to boost campaign participation.
  * Manage and respond to chats from multiple channels with [live chat](doc:live-chat).



* * *

# Instagram

## 

Why connect Instagram?

By connecting your Instagram professional account with BotBonnie, you can:

  * Automate customer service by using chatbots with [AI rules](doc:ai-rules) and different message formats. Conversations can seamlessly transition to live support agents when needed.
  * [Auto-reply](doc:auto-reply-in-instagram) to comments and mentions to boost engagement. You can also attach product links in replies to work around Instagram’s limitation of not allowing links in posts.
  * Gamify campaigns with interactive kits such as [lucky wheel](doc:lucky-wheel-kit), [scratch-off](doc:scratch-off-kit), and [daily check-in](doc:daily-checkin-kit).



> 🚧
> 
> Currently, there is an ongoing issue with Meta’s webhooks. As a result, auto replies may occasionally fail to send. Meta is working on a long-term fix. If you experience this issue, contact Appier Support to confirm if it is related.

![](https://files.readme.io/69276cb-Screen_Shot_2022-12-27_at_1.13.21_AM.png) ![](https://files.readme.io/f16494f-IG_adv_kits.png)

* * *

## 

Connect Instagram with BotBonnie

Connect your Instagram account with BotBonnie to start using your chatbot's feature such as [Instagram auto replies](doc:auto-reply-in-instagram) to engage with your customers. The connection process consists of three steps:

  1. Prepare an Instagram professional account.
  2. Connect your Instagram account to a Facebook Page.
  3. Connect your Instagram account to BotBonnie.



### 

1\. Prepare an Instagram professional account

Before connecting your Instagram account with BotBonnie, you'll need:

  * Admin access to a Facebook Page. If you don't have a Facebook Page yet, refer to [Meta's guide](https://www.facebook.com/business/help/1199464373557428?id=418112142508425) for creation instructions.
  * One of the following types of Instagram accounts:
    * A business professional account.
    * A creator professional account with less than 500,000 followers.



If you have a personal Instagram account, follow the instructions below to switch to a professional account.

#### 

Switch your Instagram account to a professional account for business

If you're using a personal account, switch to a professional account.

  1. Log in to your account, click **More > Settings > Account type and tools**, then click **Switch to professional account**.

![](https://files.readme.io/373e97e-web-1.png)

  2. In the **Which Best Describes You?** screen, choose **Business** and click **Next**. Select a category for your business, then click **Done**.

![](https://files.readme.io/3094117-web-2.png)

  3. Click **Done** to finish the process.

![](https://files.readme.io/8c1919b-web-3.png)

### 

2\. Connect your Instagram account to a Facebook Page

Log in to your Facebook Page to begin the process for connecting your Instagram account to a Facebook Page.

> 👍
> 
> For instructions on connecting a Facebook Page from the Instagram app, refer to [Instagram's guide](https://help.instagram.com/570895513091465).

  1. Click your profile picture, then go to **Settings & privacy > Settings**.

![](https://files.readme.io/5cdf052-web-4.png) ![](https://files.readme.io/1bbf59f-web-5.png)

  2. From the menu, go to **Permissions > Linked accounts**. In the row for **Instagram** , click **View** to begin the process for connecting to your Instagram account.

![](https://files.readme.io/051f197-web-6.png)

  3. Click **Connect account**.

![](https://files.readme.io/48f4956-web-7.png)

  4. In the dialogue that pops up, click **Connect**.

![](https://files.readme.io/b767ab6-web-8.png)

  5. Ensure the **Allow access to Instagram messages in Inbox** setting is enabled, then click **Confirm**. This permission allows your Instagram account to send private messages.

![](https://files.readme.io/e21bea2-web-9.png)

  6. A success message will display after your Instagram account is successfully connected to the Facebook Page. Click **Done** to dismiss the message.

![](https://files.readme.io/b8dbe69-web-10.png)

### 

3\. Connect your Instagram account to BotBonnie

  1. Log into your BotBonnie account. Go to **Channels** , then click the **Instagram** card.

![](https://files.readme.io/86058812276e3354fdf9310e44e77220842c5e2b33570e16e8d4404b48dcd802-Connecting_an_Instagram_Account.png)
  2. Click **\+ Connect Instagram Business** to start the connection flow.


![](https://files.readme.io/d59b3e13f7c3211d48a829aa4cbc23b505ca304b8236dc0085f88f5dd83dc08e-Connecting_an_Instagram_Account_2.png)

> 📘
> 
> ### 
> 
> Note
> 
> Make sure you are logged in using a Facebook account that has administrator access to the Facebook Page connected to the Instagram account.

  3. Select the Facebook Page associated with the Instagram account, then click **Continue**.

![](https://files.readme.io/2c5db74-connect-to-bb-2.png)

  4. Select the Instagram account you want to connect with BotBonnie, then click **Continue**.

![](https://files.readme.io/56cd740-connect-to-bb-3.png)

  5. Grant all permissions to BotBonnie and click **Save**.

![](https://files.readme.io/7e926e0-connect-to-bb-4.png)

  6. Click **Got it** to return to the BotBonnie console.

![](https://files.readme.io/6890c78-connect-to-bb-5.png)

  7. On the BotBonnie console, click the verticle three dots next to your Instagram account then select **Connect** to finish the connection.

![](https://files.readme.io/4d247118f29dae1896cb53bbee5a436b198d411d0f0f0d8f651f447d4c57621d-Connecting_an_Instagram_Account_3.png)

If your account was successfully connected, you'll see a **Connected** status displayed with a green check icon.

![](https://files.readme.io/29c2701ccae57cfc50cbc9fa53fd5988a6455d67d5fc146fb01165d43d7f6d0d-Connecting_an_Instagram_Account_4.png)

The connection status will be displayed on both the **Connected accounts** list and the **Channels** page.

![](https://files.readme.io/4adc3127473a25cc3efa84f9bbca207275bd796b4b889afac5b9b090e48e7598-Connecting_an_Instagram_Account_5.png)

* * *

## 

Test your connection

To test your connection, you can create an [AI rule](doc:ai-rules), then send a message containing the keyword you specified to your Instagram professional account. If your Instagram account was successfully connected, you'll receive the reply module you selected.

![](https://files.readme.io/5fb9e00-Screen_Shot_2022-12-26_at_11.30.50_PM.png)

* * *

## 

Troubleshooting

If your Instagram account is not successfully connected to BotBonnie or your bot is not working on Instagram, here are some possible reasons.

  * Your Instagram account is not properly connected to your Facebook Page
  * Your Instagram account is not connected to the BotBonnie bot



#### 

Your Instagram account is not properly connected to your Facebook Page

Log in to your Facebook Page to check if your Instagram account is connected, and ensure that the **Allow access to Instagram messages in Inbox** is enabled.

If your Instagram account isn't connected, follow the instructions to connect the account to the Facebook Page.

![](https://files.readme.io/4d52c6c-troubleshooting-1.png)

#### 

Your Instagram account is not connected to the BotBonnie bot

On the BotBonnie console, go to **Channels > Instagram**.

  * Click **Reset permissions** and re-grant access to BotBonnie.
  * If the Instagram account is connected to another bot, you can click **Connect** to connect it to the current bot.

![](https://files.readme.io/588cad24b8ab0c57d9e39a939018c953bf8e32db1692cdf8e6d1b8849b179cc0-Connecting_an_Instagram_Account_6.png)

If your issues cannot be resolved, contact your customer success manager for assistance.

__Updated 5 months ago

* * *

# KakaoTalk

> 📘
> 
> ### 
> 
> Premium feature
> 
> This is a premium feature. Contact your customer success manager for more details.

Integrating your KakaoTalk channel with BotBonnie allows you to manage Kakao Moment and Sangdam Talk with BotBonnie.

  * **Kakao Moment** : Kakao Moment messages are one-way promotional messages (e.g. annual sale announcement) that can be sent to users who opted in. After integration, you can use BotBonnie's [broadcast](doc:broadcasting-create-broadcasts) feature to send Kakao Moment messages.
  * **Sangdam Talk** : Sangdam Talk messages are 1-on-1 conversations initiated by users. After integration, you can use BotBonnie's chat-related features to manage Sangdam Talk messages.



> 📘
> 
> ### 
> 
> Note
> 
>   * Alim Talk (transactional messages) is not supported by BotBonnie.
>   * To comply with regulations in Korea, promotional messages can only be sent to users between 8:00 AM - 9:00 PM (Korea Standard Time).
> 


To connect a KakaoTalk channel with BotBonnie:

  * Create a KakaoTalk channel if you do not have a KakaoTalk channel yet.
  * You need to have access to the KakaoTalk Channel Admin Center of your channel.



* * *

## 

Set up your KakaoTalk channels

### 

Part 1. Switch to a business KakaoTalk channel

If you already have a business KakaoTalk channel, skip to Part 2. Configure channel settings.

If your KakaoTalk channel is not a business channel, log in to [Kakao Talk Channel Admin Center](https://center-pf.kakao.com/), go to **Channel (채널) > Management (관리) > Apply Business Channel (비즈니스 채널 신청)** and click **Application (신청)**.

![](https://files.readme.io/98866cc-Screen_Shot_2023-07-18_at_6.50.32_PM.png)

You will need to submit the following certificates to Kakao and wait 3 to 5 business days for Kakao's approval.

  * Certificate of Business Registration
  * Certificate of Employment or ID card of Representative
  * Registration certificates (only for a mail-order business, medical device sales, and health functional food sales business)



For more details, see [Kakao's official guide](https://t1.daumcdn.net/biz/DM/%EB%B9%84%EC%A6%88%EB%8B%88%EC%8A%A4%20%EC%B1%84%EB%84%90%20%EC%8B%A0%EC%B2%AD%EA%B0%80%EC%9D%B4%EB%93%9C_ver1.0_%EC%99%B8%EB%B6%80%EC%9A%A9.pdf) (available in Korean only).

### 

Part 2. Configure channel settings

In the Kakao Talk Channel Admin Center, go to **Channel (채널) > Dashboard (대시보드)** and make sure the following options are enabled.

  * **Public channel (채널 공개)**
  * **Allow search (검색 허용)**
  * **1:1 chat (1:1채팅)**

![](https://files.readme.io/8ddf499-Screen_Shot_2023-07-18_at_6.52.37_PM.png)

### 

Part 3. Create a Kakao Talk developer channel (recommended)

You can create a Kakao Talk developer channel for testing. A developer channel allows you to test BotBonnie features without affecting your channel users and avoid service fees.

You need to have a business KakaoTalk channel in order to create a developer channel.

In your KakaoTalk Channel Admin Center, go to **Channel (채널)** , click **Create a channel for developers (개발자용 채널 만들기)** , and create a test channel.

![](https://files.readme.io/b1334f6-Screen_Shot_2023-07-11_at_9.26.35_PM.png)

Go to **Channel (채널) > Dashboard (대시보드)** and make sure **Public channel (채널 공개)** is enabled.

![](https://files.readme.io/7616cc7-Screen_Shot_2023-07-18_at_6.55.03_PM.png)

### 

Part 4. Provide channel information to your CSM

Contact your customer success manager and provide the following channel information.

  * **KakaoTalk channel** (your production environment)
    * **Channel name (채널 이름)**
    * **Search ID (검색용 아이디)**
  * **KakaoTalk developer channel** (if available)
    * **Channel name (채널 이름)**



You can find this information by going to **Channel (채널) > Management (관리) > Detailed settings (상세설정)** in KakaoTalk Channel Admin Center.

![](https://files.readme.io/9c77682-Screen_Shot_2023-07-18_at_7.01.09_PM.png)

Next, BotBonnie team will complete the rest of the account integration and notify you when the connection is ready.

* * *

## 

Check the connected channels

After BotBonnie team completes the integration, you can now check the connected channels on the console.

  1. Go to **Channels** on the sidebar, then click the **KakaoTalk** card.

![](https://files.readme.io/0c327114bbcb483d7eba56f46b10484425b1de10d19cc59ae07bba06bdab0472-Connecting_an_KakaoTalk_Channel.png)

You will see your connected channels with a **Connected** status displayed with a green check icon.

![](https://files.readme.io/316aff08abb373cd5d2fe156ffa2254ef24d75d403500feba47df74512f96520-Connecting_an_KakaoTalk_Channel_1.png)

* * *

## 

Test your connection

From the left menu, go to **Content > AI rules**, then click **\+ Add AI Rule**.

![](https://files.readme.io/29ee5f0ad08546ff8eec75b3b6aa21a169d94c629f19bc396767e7a82711336e-AI_rules.png)

Expand the new AI rule, enter a keyword under **User input** (e.g. `test`), select **Exact match** , select a module to be the **Bot reply** , and click **Deploy bot**. The keyword should be something your users are unlikely to enter when they send a message to your KakaoTalk channel.

![](https://files.readme.io/89ebaa7-Screen_Shot_2023-07-18_at_6.07.02_PM.png)

Send a message to your KakaoTalk channel that exactly matches the keyword in the AI rule and you should see the module you selected as the reply.

# LINE

Connect your LINE Official Account (OA) to BotBonnie to engage with users through automated chatbot interactions. This integration allows you to automate responses, manage conversations, and enhance customer engagement on LINE.

To connect your LINE OA:

  * Ensure you have admin access to your [LINE OA](https://manager.line.biz/) and [LINE Developers console](https://developers.line.biz/console/).
  * Your Messaging API and LINE Login channels must be under the same LINE provider.



You can also enable live agent chatroom to seamlessly switch between automated and human customer agent support.

* * *

## 

Connect a LINE OA to BotBonnie

  1. Log into your BotBonnie account. Go to **Channels** , then click the **LINE** card.

![](https://files.readme.io/99c5b3e9ed9bbc84fd12bb7299b041b01f9e70ad85078c7eaa82cea5309a0117-Connecting_a_LINE_Official_Account_1.png)
  2. Click **\+ Connect LINE OA** to start the connection flow.


![](https://files.readme.io/de876cffe9938bcee582dca46387410432cb07dc8a9934d7bf7e4a11120e309a-Connecting_a_LINE_Official_Account_2.png)

### 

Part I. Connect Messaging API channel

  1. Log in to [LINE Developers Console](https://developers.line.biz/console/), go to the provider of your LINE OA, and check if there's already a Messaging API channel.

     1. If there's no Messaging API channel under the provider, see how to create a Messaging API channel.
     2. If a Messaging API channel exists and you have admin access, return to BotBonnie's console and click **Continue** to complete **① Create Messaging API channel** and **② Enable Messaging API**.

![](https://files.readme.io/e9d41f8-LINE_channels_API.png)

  2. On LINE Developers console, click the Messaging API channel and go to the **Basic settings** tab.

     1. Find **Channel ID** , click the copy icon, and paste the **Channel ID** on BotBonnie console under **1\. Connect a Messaging API channel > ③ Set up channel ID**, and click **Continue**.

![](https://files.readme.io/b92f6ad7988606ced8db68e10794028b848e58b3055e6ee7c671bcb88786b7df-line-developer-channel-id.png) ![](https://files.readme.io/ac4434c0a5d2c6f355164618a4beb238006f9c40abf0a58abe9b2cbadea36cb0-channel-id-bb.png)

ii. Copy the **Channel secret** from LINE Developers console, paste it on BotBonnie console under **1\. Connect a Messaging API channel > ④ Set up channel secret**, and click **Continue**.

![](https://files.readme.io/1aa14e18b16b0ec96f62862caf18fbdd73dadb11de61ce9525620ff53ed07ef6-line-developer-channel-secret.png) ![](https://files.readme.io/3c28dda4eb5c0dd79e24434700596c61e457586a6e61594f9e5ae9510c2525b4-channel-secret-bb.png)

  3. On LINE Developers console, go to the **Messaging API** tab in the Messaging API channel.
     1. Find the **Channel access token** , click **Issue** if empty, copy the channel access token, paste it on BotBonnie console under **1\. Connect a Messaging API channel > ⑤ Set up channel access token**, and click **Continue**.

![](https://files.readme.io/7abca7804ad4adb7c2d3884e1046a8f716812986cdbc976f556b6db4f58f704e-access-token-bb.png) ![](https://files.readme.io/f627d50975588d2abfcdb4f8690c77df27ae8c10dda081744ec532e9960fd6dd-channel-access-token-bb.png)

  4. Reload the LINE Developers console. Go to **Messaging API** > **Webhook settings** , and enable **Use webhook** , followed by **Webhook redelivery** and **Error statistic aggregation**.

![](https://files.readme.io/994dddd1b813200fdfefbcedfdffdef1817605ed21d14b5f8c891e898a727531-webhook-enabled.png)

  5. Go back to BotBonnie console, click **Continue** under **⑥ Set up Webhook**.
  6. Log in to [LINE Official Account Manager](https://manager.line.biz/), select the LINE OA you're configuring, click **Settings** > **Response settings**.
     1. Disable **Chat** , **Greeting message** , and **Auto-response messages**.
     2. Make sure **Webhooks** is enabled.

![](https://files.readme.io/8a212a8-Screen_Shot_2023-04-07_at_6.58.14_PM.png)

  7. Return to the BotBonnie console. Click **Connect login channel** to complete the LINE login channel setup.

![](https://files.readme.io/1e28fe570ccb53e7dd76ab57aeae292948511e4fd28949cfe94ff702c2c315c3-step7-LINEOA-settings.png)

### 

Part II. Connect LINE Login channel

  8. On LINE Developers console, go to the same provider as your Messaging API channel, and check if there's already a LINE Login channel.


  * If you don't see a LINE Login channel under the same provider with the Messaging API channel, see how to create a LINE Login channel.
  * If there's a LINE Login channel, make sure you have admin access to the channel. You can skip **2\. Connect a Login channel > ① Create Login channel** on BotBonnie console.

![](https://files.readme.io/700367b-LINE_channels_Login.png)

  9. On LINE Developers console, click the LINE Login channel and go to the **Basic settings** tab.
     1. Find the **Channel ID** , copy the channel ID, paste it on BotBonnie console under **2\. Connect a Login channel > ② Set up channel ID**, and click **Continue**.

![](https://files.readme.io/77953c28a39c6e85a966f91875757a9e3501ca628e63a81a863e55710ff8694c-login-channel-id.png) ![](https://files.readme.io/6d5f038426378be7c0f39f88f99609f142764412d074bc016be8a1180e1e10ed-login-channel-id-bb.png)

ii. Find **Channel secret** , copy the channel secret, paste it on BotBonnie console under **2\. Connect a Login channel > ③ Set up channel secret**, and click **Complete**.

![](https://files.readme.io/003fd4dd36708161b6f162fbe2c1a83128265c279f32ab1d49f2a09cdfe2b0ee-login-channel-secret.png) ![](https://files.readme.io/9c578b6a41f3c8e3762439fdf0018d6741409ebdbdfc3673073e891570af935d-login-channel-bb.png)

  10. If the login channel is not published yet, scroll up to the top of the LINE Login channel, click **Developing** and click **Publish**.

![](https://files.readme.io/ab3d914-Screen_Shot_2023-04-10_at_8.54.44_AM.png) ![](https://files.readme.io/1f65266-Screen_Shot_2023-04-10_at_8.55.20_AM.png)

If your account was successfully connected, you'll see a **Connected** status displayed with a green check icon.

![](https://files.readme.io/138bd47baba1052b50e5c3fab2b7f6dfd1c6fbfc783bfe5d0042f80c572a10dd-Connecting_a_LINE_Official_Account_3_1.png)

The connection status will be displayed on both the **Connected accounts** list and the **Channels** page.

![](https://files.readme.io/6b0083d0493d6e2216f0c2647832f6b7dadb3a9bc8a0b49be091e40d357e3015-Connecting_a_LINE_Official_Account_4.png)

### 

Test your connection

  1. From the left menu, go to **Content** > **AI rules** , then click **\+ Add AI Rule**.

![](https://files.readme.io/5e7f92ef67597d2d6d459ab5c015d855f067e271137be4a66eb292b1efe1d5c1-AI_rules.png)

  2. Expand the new AI rule and complete the following settings.
     1. Enter a keyword under **User input** (such as `test`). The keyword should be something your users are unlikely to enter when they send a message to your LINE OA.
     2. Under **Condition** , select **Exact match**.
     3. Under **Bot reply** , select an advanced kit, such as a lucky wheel, daily check-in, or scratch-off kit.
     4. Click **Deploy bot**.

![](https://files.readme.io/4ffbe52-Screen_Shot_2023-04-10_at_7.41.48_PM.png)

  3. Send a message to your LINE OA that exactly matches the keyword in the AI rule and you should see the advanced kit you selected as the reply.

![](https://files.readme.io/36b112e-Screen_Shot_2023-04-11_at_2.16.20_PM.png)

  4. Run the advanced kit to make sure it works.

![](https://files.readme.io/b119efd-Screen_Shot_2023-04-11_at_2.18.41_PM.png)

If errors occur while running the advanced kit, the LINE Login channel might not be connected properly. See how to troubleshoot.

### 

Troubleshooting

Here are some common issues you might run into while setting up your LINE OA with BotBonnie:

  * I can't find the Messaging API Channel
  * I can't find the LINE Login Channel
  * I can't click the Complete button on BotBonnie console
  * Your LINE OA is connected to another BotBonnie bot
  * Advanced kits and growth tools are not working properly



#### 

I can't find the Messaging API Channel

If you cannot find the Messaging API channel under the provider on LINE Official Account Manager, log into [LINE Developers Console](https://developers.line.biz/console/), and go to **Settings > Messaging API** to check if a Messaging API channel is already created for the LINE OA.

![](https://files.readme.io/38baa58-LINEOA_Settings_MsgAPI.png)

If the Messaging API channel is enabled already but not visible on LINE Developers console, it's possible that you do not have access to it. Check with your team to request access to the Messaging API channel. To see how to grant channel access, refer to [LINE's documentation](https://developers.line.biz/en/docs/line-developers-console/managing-roles/#role-settings-for-channel).

![](https://files.readme.io/9fdcece-LINEOA_MsgAPI_Enabled.png)

If Messaging API is not enabled, click **Enable Messaging API** and select the provider of the LINE OA to enable. This creates a Messaging API channel for your LINE OA.

![](https://files.readme.io/5e466c0-LINEOA_MessagingAPI.png) ![](https://files.readme.io/a726014-LINEOA_MsgAPI_Enabled.png)

#### 

I can't find the LINE Login Channel

If you cannot find the LINE Login channel under the provider on LINE Developers console, click **Create a new channel** and select **LINE Login** to create a login channel.

![](https://files.readme.io/b9fd6eb-LINE_newchannel.png) ![](https://files.readme.io/aae9766-LINE_newlogin.png)

Set **App types** to **Web app** and complete all other settings.

![](https://files.readme.io/8ae6865-Screen_Shot_2023-04-07_at_7.23.44_PM.png)

Click **Create**.

![](https://files.readme.io/d5aa442-Screen_Shot_2023-04-10_at_9.44.10_AM.png)

#### 

I can't click the Complete button on BotBonnie console

If you have reached the final step on BotBonnie console and the **Complete** button is grayed out, this means that some steps are not completed yet.

![](https://files.readme.io/25eb35c-line-complete-button.png)

Make sure both parts are marked as 100% completed.

![](https://files.readme.io/23cbe66-Screen_Shot_2023-04-10_at_11.18.05_AM.png)

Each step should have a green checkmark next to it. If not, go back to that step, make sure the field is entered correctly and click **Continue**.

![](https://files.readme.io/33a8ebee82d168de236c7e825578f8bcc10e63e259df111664ee0d81abed5c33-Connecting_a_LINE_Official_Account_-_I_cant_click_the_Complete_button_on_BotBonnie_Console.png)

#### 

Your LINE OA is connected to another BotBonnie bot

If the LINE OA is connected to another bot, you can click the vertical three dots next to the LINE OA and select **Connect** to connect it to the current bot.

![](https://files.readme.io/26538c4b251d267f2fea6245ca899da59f9f1562bac25c26141b39ff98148865-Connecting_a_LINE_Official_Account_5.png)

#### 

Advanced kits and growth tools are not working properly

If your advanced kits, growth tools, or referral links are not working, the most common cause is that the Messaging API and LINE Login channels are set up under different providers, which prevents the same user ID from being assigned to the same user.

To check this, go to your [LINE Developers Console](https://developers.line.biz/console/) and verify that both the Messaging API channel and LINE Login channel are listed under the same provider.

![](https://files.readme.io/8693a4c-LINE_channels_provider.png)

Next, make sure the channel ID and channel secret for the LINE Login channel are entered correctly in BotBonnie. Go to **Channels** > **LINE** , click the vertical three dots next to the connected LINE OA, and select **Settings**.

![](https://files.readme.io/c71830dceae9103eaaf2ac1a8b6bf143a7301d7fc1c4afc03d59fe7a093bf669-Connecting_a_LINE_Official_Account_6.png)

If your channels are under different providers, create a new channel under the provider that's already associated with your connected LINE OA.

If your issues cannot be resolved, contact your customer success manager for assistance.

* * *

## 

Integrate with other Appier products

You can integrate your LINE Official Account in BotBonnie with your other Appier products like AIQUA and AiDeal to enhance your engagement strategies. To set up any integration, contact your customer success manager for assistance.

After the integration is completed, you can manage your integration settings by going to **Channels** > **LINE** , clicking the vertical three dots next to the connected LINE OA, and clicking **App Integration**.

![](https://files.readme.io/862a8258368d90eb16790a60363704e381c5f9687406ac9d9874329c7e922d7f-app-integration.png)

* * *

## 

Enable live agent chatroom for LINE

The BotBonnie [live agent chatroom](doc:live-chat#live-agent-chatroom) lets you manage one-on-one user interactions on LINE without incurring messaging fees. When users enter the chatroom, it opens in a LIFF web view within the LINE app.

Follow the steps below to enable this feature:

  1. Go to **Channels** on the sidebar and click the **LINE** card.

![](https://files.readme.io/d3b987b585013be094ee7483e6ef4d10d2409d7b4b02d567d7c5416240bab8c7-Connecting_a_LINE_Official_Account_12.png)

  2. Click the vertical three dots next to the LINE OA and select **Settings**.

![](https://files.readme.io/11b46e8ea97e4693b0490dbf9b0dc1d6bc4e451703f804e96a7b70830d92cbe7-Connecting_a_LINE_Official_Account_8.png)

  3. Next, click on **Live agent chatroom** tab, toggle the **Live agent chatroom** switch to allow your live agent to chat with users.

![](https://files.readme.io/c02222c8a8b1d71fb8a8cfeafadb5432589ffc556cd736c5f2e48d59186d5237-Connecting_a_LINE_Official_Account_9.png)

  4. Enter a chatroom name to appear at the top of the chatroom.
  5. Enable the **Chatroom announcement** to display a message at the top of conversation when needed.

![](https://files.readme.io/5db5b3371aa6cbec4e85953489e81dbd407c2f39c3180dc7ce83727064b8de75-Connecting_a_LINE_Official_Account_10.png)

  6. Turn on the **Unread message reminder** and set it up to remind users when they haven't read a message for a specified period of time.
     1. Set the reminder period and select a time unit (minutes, hours, or days) from the dropdown.
     2. Configure the **Reminder module** to define the message users receive when reminded.



> 📘
> 
> ### 
> 
> Note
> 
> The unread message reminder is sent via a LINE OA and may incur LINE messaging fees. Customer service agents can [manage the unread message reminder](doc:live-chat#managing-unread-messages-in-your-chatroom) for each live chat chatroom as needed.

![](https://files.readme.io/68b76455b5935698aff7bde76cb361f67e0991634642165efc8295bf6a88af44-Connecting_a_LINE_Official_Account_11.png)

After enabling this feature, you can add the button to [**chatbot flows**](doc:triggered-actions#chatbot-flows-buttons-quick-replies-and-user-input) by selecting **Open live agent chatroom** on click action and initiating live agent chatroom within live chat.

Powered by [](https://readme.com?ref_src=hub&project=botbonnie-resource-center)

# Share with LINE Friend

> 👍
> 
> ### 
> 
> Supported channels
> 
> The following messaging platforms support the **Share with LINE friend** button option:
> 
>   * Instagram
>   * Messenger
>   * LINE
>   * WebChat
> 


The **Share with LINE friend** [button](doc:button) option allows a user to share a message with their LINE friends, groups, and chats, making it simpler than ever to spread the word and increase reach for your brand.

### 

Message limitations

The messages in the shared module must be one of the following supported types:

Supported message type| Limitations  
---|---  
[Text](doc:message-type-text)| • Buttons used in text messages only support the **Open URL** and **Phone call** options. • Triggered actions aren't supported.  
[Carousel](doc:message-type-carousel)| • Buttons used in carousel messages only the support the **Open URL** and **Phone call** options. • Triggered actions aren't supported.  
[Audio](doc:message-type-audio)| Triggered actions aren't supported.  
[Image](doc:message-type-image)| Triggered actions aren't supported.  
[Video](doc:message-type-video)| Triggered actions aren't supported.  
  
* * *

## 

Setup steps

### 

1\. (For LINE channels) Enable **`shareTargetPicker`**

If you're sending the **Share with LINE friend** button in a LINE channel, you'll need to enable the **`shareTargetPicker`** LIFF setting before using this feature.

In the LINE Developers Console, go to the LINE Login channel associated with the connected page. Under the **LIFF** tab, enable the **`shareTargetPicker`** toggle:

![](https://files.readme.io/0270f40-line-sharetargetpicker.png)

### 

2\. Add a button to a message

In your chatbot flows, add a button to one of your messages. From the **On click** dropdown menu in the button settings, select **Share with LINE friend**.

![](https://files.readme.io/0b78235-on-click.png)

### 

3\. Select the shared message module

From the dropdown menu that appears, select the module you'd like the user to share. Ensure that the messages in the module you select don't violate the message limitations.

![](https://files.readme.io/85b67d4-line-shared-message.png)

When you've finished setting up the button to deploy your changes.

# Messenger

Connect your Messenger account to BotBonnie to engage with your audience using automated chatbot interactions. This integration allows you to automate responses, manage interactions, and optimize your marketing efforts on Messenger.

To connect your Messenger account:

  1. [Create a Facebook Page](https://www.facebook.com/pages/create) if you don’t already have one.
  2. Ensure you have admin access to your Facebook Page.



You can also integrate the Meta Conversion API to enhance tracking and optimize campaigns with detailed user interactions.

* * *

## 

Connect a Messenger account

Follow the instructions below to connect your Messenger account to BotBonnie.

  1. Log into your BotBonnie account. Go to **Channels** , then click the **Messenger** card.

![](https://files.readme.io/ce0d1122c28dd242e1c1aa49f06e8be1be074d22d96342608254b25ba305c405-Connecting_a_Facebook_Page_1.png)

  2. Click **\+ Connect Messenger** to start the connection flow.

![](https://files.readme.io/6d5aa570e781e227b3387f9fa19f139709bdd20b6c3e0e90414e055d55a40de0-Connecting_a_Facebook_Page_2.png)

  3. Log in using a Facebook account that has admin access or full control of the Facebook Page associated with the Messenger account you want to connect.
  4. Select the Facebook Page linked to the Messenger account you want to connect with BotBonnie and click **Continue**.

![](https://files.readme.io/73a994ce97bb15cf4879644bef434c78e20339c292fdcf532a79b81934678022-Frame_1321317055.png)

  5. Select the Businesses you want to connect to BotBonnie and click **Continue**.

![](https://files.readme.io/960d4cca214945617d765bf9a06dbe6b8e29a3b948f16321710d65ac7cada258-Frame_1321317057.png)

  6. On the BotBonnie console, click the vertical three dots next to your Messenger account, then select **Connect** to finish the connection.

![](https://files.readme.io/ee5ee6c8a95482d287a3e1bdd7fef474f7f6ee326ef9ad3a1249ef9bba0beed3-Connecting_a_Facebook_Page_3.png)

If your account was successfully connected, you'll see a **Connected** status displayed with a green check icon.

![](https://files.readme.io/2dcf9af277dcb5985f11ea5cb53bb7977fa87ee2d4bd7e66a3995a15721015a0-Connecting_a_Facebook_Page_4.png)

The connection status will be displayed on both the **Connected accounts** list and the **Channels** page.

![](https://files.readme.io/ef3ad7e7e70a0029a28e1abe179f73f5e08887546914b06cd355753e05beb0a3-Connecting_a_Facebook_Page_5.png)

> 👍
> 
> ### 
> 
> Tip
> 
> For advanced data tracking, Enable the Meta Conversion API to send event data to Meta and enhance your tracking and advertising capabilities.

### 

Test your connection

From the left menu, go to **Content > AI rules**, then click **\+ Add AI Rule**.

![](https://files.readme.io/aaa1b17911ca757cc657656d6718dcf1ce26a53dd4db2a279aa898b6adee9240-ai-rule.png)

Expand the new AI rule, enter a keyword under **User input** (e.g. `test`), select **Exact match** , select a module to be the **Bot reply** , and click **Deploy bot**. The keyword should be something your users are unlikely to enter when they send a message to your Messenger account.

![](https://files.readme.io/4ef59e9-BB_NewRule.png)

Send a message to your Messenger account that exactly matches the keyword in the AI rule and you should see the module you selected as the reply.

![](https://files.readme.io/b96cf25-BB_FBTestResults.png)

### 

Troubleshooting

If your Facebook Page is not successfully connected to BotBonnie or your bot is not working on the Facebook Page, here are some possible reasons.

  * Third-party cookies are blocked on your browser
  * Your Facebook account needs full control or admin access of the associated Facebook Page
  * Your Messenger account is not properly connected to the BotBonnie bot



#### 

Third-party cookies are blocked on your browser

Check the settings of your browser to make sure third-party cookies are not blocked. We'll use Chrome as an example.

Open Chrome, click the menu button in the top-right corner, and go to **Settings > Privacy and security > Cookies and other site data**.

![](https://files.readme.io/e8fc23c-BB_BrowserSetting.png)

Make sure third-party cookies are not blocked.

![](https://files.readme.io/fe298c0-BB_BrowserCookies.png)

#### 

Your Facebook account needs full control or admin access of the associated Facebook Page

If you're using the New Pages Experiences, your Facebook account needs to have full control of the Facebook Page.

![](https://files.readme.io/6d88ba3-BB_FBFullAccess.png)

If you're using Classic Pages, your Facebook account needs to be an Admin of the Facebook Page.

![](https://files.readme.io/074b2f2-BB_FBAdminAccess.png)

Here's how Classic Page roles compare to the access levels in the new Pages experience:

Classic Page roles| Page access in the New Pages Experience  
---|---  
Admin| Facebook access with full control  
Editor| Facebook access with partial control  
Moderator| Task access for Message Replies, Community Activity, Ads, Insights  
Advertiser| Task access for Ads, Insights  
Analyst| Task access for Insights  
Community manager| Community manager access to moderate Live chats  
  
To see how to check the access level of your Facebook account, refer to [Facebook Help Center](https://www.facebook.com/help/510247025775149/).

#### 

Your Messenger account is not properly connected to the BotBonnie bot

On the BotBonnie console, go to **Channels** > **Messenger**.

  * Click **Reset permissions** and re-grant access to BotBonnie.

![](https://files.readme.io/bfc174716159aaf6a726eb11a9e273072f9a5d3406aa019d631ddddc9280c6a7-Connecting_a_Facebook_Page_6.png)

  * If the Messenger account is connected to another bot, you can click **Connect** to connect it to the current bot.

![](https://files.readme.io/4809d773ce73c2b74d3981dc8e18ba876f8747f0bb2883fcbe45f8e2faf2dd4c-Connecting_a_Facebook_Page_7.png)

If your issues cannot be resolved, contact your customer success manager for assistance.

* * *

## 

Enable the Meta Conversion API integration

The Meta Conversion API allows you to track key customer events in Messenger, such as adding products to cart, purchasing, initiating checkouts, and submitting leads for better advertising campaign optimization.

Ensure you have full access permissions to enable the integration for the Messenger account you want to connect.

  1. If your bot hasn't connected to a Messenger account, click **\+ Connect Messenger** to connect a Messenger account.

![](https://files.readme.io/bf1d74fd063dc422b24f7be83567067b55d6f04857f47ad45f14000b0469b9f8-Connecting_a_Facebook_Page_8.png)

  2. For the Messenger account you'd like to enable the Conversion API for, click the vertical three dots, then select **Connect Meta Conversion API**.

![](https://files.readme.io/6d03f5e786d57f52964b4fa9635f027a4acf9bea48efd30344db97b7c9c2f9d7-Connecting_a_Facebook_Page_9.png)

  3. You'll be redirected to Meta's authorization page.

     1. Select the appropriate business account and Facebook page associated with your Messenger account.
     2. In the final step, make sure you see the "Log events on your Page's behalf and send those events to Facebook" and click **Save**.
![](https://files.readme.io/b6ae5e0337d86bbd8aa6b3615c71e220c683fc1bf7c1cedaac0cd20a096f6646-authentication.png)



> 📘
> 
> ### 
> 
> Note
> 
> Skip the Instagram and WhatsApp account selections, as they are not supported for this integration.

  4. After completing the connection, check the BotBonnie console to verify the connection status:


  * **Connected (Conversion API)** indicates a successful connection.
  * **Error** suggests a connection error.

![](https://files.readme.io/f85b769fca3e8cfc89e7a0a7635b2c1204d62e0e31f5fe3b0fbedabb65329762-Connecting_a_Facebook_Page_10.png)

> 🚧
> 
> ### 
> 
> Troubleshooting Conversion API connection error
> 
> If you encounter an error while connecting your bot with the Meta Conversion API, check that:
> 
>   * You have full page access.
>   * A dataset has been created in Meta Business Suite. To learn more, see [Create a dataset during messaging event set creation](https://www.facebook.com/business/help/5818684664831465?id=490360542427371).
> 


Once the Conversion API integration is complete, you'll be able to [send conversion events to Meta using triggered actions in buttons](doc:triggered-actions#send-conversion-event-to-meta).

# Share on Facebook

> 👍
> 
> ### 
> 
> Supported channels
> 
> The following messaging platforms support the **Share on Facebook** button option:
> 
>   * Instagram
>   * Facebook
>   * LINE
>   * WebChat
> 


The **Share on Facebook** [button](doc:button) option allow users to share a URL on their Facebook wall, creating the opportunity for their friends and followers to see your promotional content and marketing messages.

![](https://files.readme.io/b491281-share-on-facebook-message.png)

Users who click on this button will be redirected to Facebook, where they can share the Facebook post with the message content you configured:

![](https://files.readme.io/27f4eb3-share-on-facebook.png)

* * *

## 

Setup steps

  1. Go to your chatbot flows and add a button to one of your messages. From the **On click** dropdown menu in the button settings, select **Share on Facebook**.

![](https://files.readme.io/4e6af47-on-click-share-on-facebook.png)

  2. In the modal that appears, configure the **Shared URL** and set the **Action after sharing**.
     1. **Shared URL** : The URL to be shared in the Facebook post.
     2. **Action after sharing** : The action to be executed after the user finishes sharing the post.
        1. **Postback module** : Send a module in the chat.
        2. **Open URL** : Redirect the user to a specific URL.

![](https://files.readme.io/459651d-shared-url-action-after-sharing.png)

  3. Next, provide the content for the Facebook post, such as the posts hashtag, title, and subtitle.
     1. Hashtag: Must not exceed 20 characters.
     2. Image: File size must not exceed 1 MB. Supported file formats: PNG, JPEG, GIF. To prevent the image from being cropped, we recommend using an aspect ratio of 1.91:1.
     3. Title: Must be 3 to 80 characters.
     4. Subtitle: Must not exceed 100 characters.

![](https://files.readme.io/e6e06e2-message-content.png)

  4. Click **Close** , then deploy your changes.

# Viber

Integrate your bot with Viber to send [broadcasts](doc:broadcasting-create-broadcasts) to your users using their phone number. To learn how to get started, contact your customer success manager.

* * *

## 

Send Viber broadcast

> 👍
> 
> ### 
> 
> Tip
> 
> See [Broadcasts](doc:broadcasting-create-broadcasts) to how to create and send broadcasts.

### 

Supported message types

The following message types are supported for Viber broadcasts:

  * **Text (Viber)** : Consists of a text-only message. Can contain one button.
  * **Image (Viber)** : Consists of a title and an image. Can contain one button.

![](https://files.readme.io/b701a5999a4c94061f2697cdc67d23a1e1ad6c47908f32832d38fded7b8321a2-viber-message-types.png)

### 

Phone number requirements

  * Broadcasts can only be sent to users who have a phone number saved to the **Mobile** field in their profile. Users in the broadcast segment without a saved phone number won't receive the broadcast.
  * To avoid delivery issues, phone numbers should include the country code, including the leading `+`.

![](https://files.readme.io/b98440eba0b96638a24f7891d696e81f64ef0195746b10db158b4cd3d4b68c2d-user-profile-mobile-number.png)

# WebChat

WebChat is BotBonnie's chat widget that can be installed on your website. Using WebChat, you can:

  * Utilize BotBonnie features to automate customer services
  * Customize the look and feel of your WebChat widget
  * Control the web pages where you want the WebChat to be displayed
  * Let users link to their accounts on other messaging platforms

![](https://files.readme.io/0edc70f-Screen_Shot_2023-12-26_at_7.52.39_PM.png)   


* * *

## 

Set up WebChat

  1. Log into your BotBonnie account. Go to **Channels** , then click the **WebChat** card.

![](https://files.readme.io/0d7dbee85ef2e56e1e6976e454d9a1c65cb065af9b78b88aeea62982c2319380-WebChat_integration_1_1.png)

  2. Click **Set up** to start the connection flow.

![](https://files.readme.io/c4a40cb1da6586e9b976e256026ba7951d367b8dd59d78ebc3ca62118d0f85c0-WebChat_integration_2.png)

You will need to set up the following sections on the console, and then install the WebChat code to your website.

  * Step 1. Basic info
  * Step 2. Website URL
  * Step 3. Customer service slots
  * Step 4. Messaging platform linking
  * Step 5. Installation



### 

Step 1. Basic info

#### 

Basic information

  * **Brand name** : Type the name of your business. This is the name that will be displayed to the user.
  * **Welcome message (optional)** : The welcome message is displayed at the top of the WebChat home page.



> 📘
> 
> ### 
> 
> Note
> 
> If you have selected a Getting Started module in **Content > Greetings**, this greeting message will be displayed before the user starts a conversation with you.

![](https://files.readme.io/d9fc08d-Screen_Shot_2023-12-26_at_8.29.55_PM.png)

#### 

WebChat widget settings

  * **Brand logo** : Click **Upload image** and upload a logo image in JPG / PNG format that's less than 2 MB.
  * **Brand color** : Choose the theme color of your WebChat.

![](https://files.readme.io/04547ec-webchat-widget-settings.png)

#### 

Launcher settings

Launcher is the chat icon that will be displayed on your website.

  * **Launcher preview** : When you are setting up the launcher, you can enter the URL of your website and click Preview to see what the launcher will look like on the website. Depending on the URL you input, it will take up to 3~20 seconds to load your website.
  * **Launcher image** : Click **Upload image** and upload an image that will be the chat icon shown to users.
  * **Launcher position** : Select to align the launcher to the bottom-left or bottom-right corner of the browser window.
  * **Launcher margins** : Set the spacing between your launcher and the edges of the browser window. You can set different margins for mobile devices. Depending on the **Launcher position** selected above, the bottom-left or bottom-right edges will be used for alignment.

![](https://files.readme.io/b7b0967-Screen_Shot_2023-12-26_at_8.26.53_PM.png)

#### 

Chat window settings

  * **Enable input fields in the chat window** : If no agent is available, you can choose to hide input fields in the chat window.
  * **Skip the homepage and open the chat window directly** : When enabled, the homepage will be hidden. Users will not be able to see the chat options for the social platforms set up under **Messaging platform linking**.

![](https://files.readme.io/ee14772-Screen_Shot_2023-12-26_at_8.29.14_PM.png)

When done, click **Next** to proceed to the next tab.

### 

Step 2. Website URL

You can control whether you want to display the WebChat widget on every page of the website or only on certain pages.

  * **Show the WebChat widget on every page** : The WebChat widget will be shown on every webpage with the WebChat snippet installed.
  * **Only show the WebChat widget on pages matching your URL rules** : The WebChat widget will be shown on web pages that match any of the URL rules you set. Click **Add URL rule** , select one of the operators below, and enter a URL (or part of a URL).
    * `is`: The widget will be shown if the webpage URL is an exact match of what you have entered.
    * `contains`: The widget will be shown if the webpage URL contains what you have entered.

![](https://files.readme.io/f4b8456ae1843b29f7ddc9b797822b93702c5bbfc9d4652ade2ba700a0dc04c2-WebChat_integration_3.png)

When done, click **Next** to proceed to the next tab.

### 

Step 3. Customer service slots

The business hours and agent off-hours configurations in the Settings page are applied to WebChat. Make sure they are set up properly.

  * **Business hours** : To set up the business hours, click **Go edit** and you will be taken to the **Settings > Business hours** page. During business hours, an **Online** icon will be displayed in the WebChat. Outside of business hours, an **Offline** icon will be displayed.
  * **Agent off-hours** : To set up agent off-hours and autoreply, click **Go edit** and you'll be taken to the **Settings > Notify agent** page. If enabled, when the user triggers the **Notfy agent** action outside of business hours:
    * An autoreply will be sent to remind your users that agents will respond during business hours.
    * Notifications through LINE Notify will be paused, while email notifications will still be sent.

![](https://files.readme.io/f85f571-Screen_Shot_2023-12-26_at_8.04.59_PM.png)

When done, click **Next** to proceed to the next tab.

### 

Step 4. Messaging platform linking

You can let users contact you via other messaging platforms such as Facebook Messenger, LINE, and WhatsApp. By doing so, the users’ browsers will be linked with their accounts on the messaging platforms, allowing you to identify the same user across different platforms.

When users click on the messaging platform in WebChat, they will be directed to send a message to your channel on that platform. Upon successful linking, a success message will be sent to the users.

![](https://files.readme.io/ab32c5d-Screen_Shot_2023-12-26_at_6.50.34_PM.png)

To set up, select the messaging platforms you want to enable and complete the following settings.

  * **Description** : Enter a short sentence to let users know that they can chat with you through this platform. You can also provide incentives to encourage users to complete linking.
  * **Channel** : Select the channel to link to.
  * **Message of successful linking** : Set the module that will be sent to the users upon successful linking. You can use this to introduce the services available on the platform or to provide the incentives you have offered to users.
  * **Triggered actions** : Select any actions you want to trigger upon successful linking.

![](https://files.readme.io/9ab93f2-Screen_Shot_2023-12-26_at_7.00.08_PM.png)

Click **Next** to proceed to the next tab.

### 

Step 5. Installation

On Step 5. Installation, click **Done**.

![](https://files.readme.io/8f8316505500bcc4aa37c978d6a369d80e023c72787de68e7e03edd1a431600d-WebChat_integration_4.png)

You will see the page ID of the WebChat shown on the console. In the next section, you will use this page ID to install the WebChat code snippet to your website.

![](https://files.readme.io/26b1fa2e814e913c0c2a406c15bc6cc51fc8d7ec189113b4b29bb6853198af7c-WebChat_integration_5.png)

* * *

## 

Install the WebChat code

Now you need to install the WebChat code snippet to every page of your website. In the instructions below, we will use Google Tag Manager to complete the installation.

  1. Copy the page ID of your WebChat bot.

![](https://files.readme.io/9e2504de253d0484b8e73ec115304f6c1c64e64deb70a974253cfa8e3dbade60-WebChat_integration_5.png)

  2. Use the copied page ID to replace the `<YOUR_WEBCHAT_PAGEID>` part in the code snippets below.



WebChat code
    
    
    <script>
      window.BBAsyncInit = function() {
        BB.init({
          appId: "<YOUR_WEBCHAT_PAGEID>"
        });
      };
    </script>
    <script async type="text/javascript" src="https://asset.botbonnie.com/sdk/sdk.js"></script>
    

> 📘
> 
> ### 
> 
> Note
> 
> If you are not using Google Tag Manager, skip the steps below and add the code snippet before the end of the `</body>` tag on every page of your website.

  3. Log into your Google Tag Manager account, click **Tags** , and click **New** to create a new tag.

![](https://files.readme.io/436e731-newtag.png)

  4. Rename the tag (e.g. WebChat Tag), click the edit icon in Tag Configuration, and select **Custom HTML**.

![](https://files.readme.io/c1ab77c-edittag.png) ![](https://files.readme.io/23d251c-customHTML.png)

  5. Under HTML, paste the code snippet from step 2.



> 👍
> 
> ### 
> 
> Tip
> 
> Make sure you have replaced the page ID.

![](https://files.readme.io/d9c56d6727234dce10e8b7d27d3d28dbc8213b3668a519f7895ab62fdd8a92aa-WebChat_integration_-_code_snippet.png)

  6. Click the edit icon in **Triggering** , select **All Pages** , and click **Save**.

![](https://files.readme.io/f6e9dbe-Screen_Shot_2023-12-26_at_9.46.33_AM.png) ![](https://files.readme.io/a0f9a98-Screen_Shot_2023-12-26_at_9.48.37_AM.png)

  7. Click **Preview** and enter your website URL.

![](https://files.readme.io/c1b5fe0-TagPreview.png)

Make sure the WebChat tag you created is firing properly.

![](https://files.readme.io/9a277ae-DebugMode.png)

  8. When you’re done checking, click **Submit > Publish**.

![](https://files.readme.io/6860725-TagPreview_copy.png)

You should see the WebChat widget on your website.

![](https://files.readme.io/27244a0-webchat_widget.png)

* * *

## 

Test your integration

From the left menu, go to **Content > AI rules**, then click **\+ Add AI Rule**.

![](https://files.readme.io/f39fba2cc48e856baaa378f715a0dc6247d39d0aeb729fbe449375ead3daa1e6-AI_rules.png)

Expand the new AI rule, enter a keyword under **User input** (e.g. `test`), select **Exact match** , select a module to be the **Bot reply** , and click **Deploy bot**. The keyword should be something your users are unlikely to enter when they send a message to you.

![](https://files.readme.io/89ebaa7-Screen_Shot_2023-07-18_at_6.07.02_PM.png)

Send a message to your WebChat that exactly matches the keyword in the AI rule and you should see the module you selected as the reply.

![](https://files.readme.io/3990fd1-Screen_Shot_2023-12-26_at_7.14.03_PM.png)

__Updated 6 months ago

* * *

# Starting a Chat in a Web View

Use in-app web views to open a WebChat bot in your mobile app. A web view allows you to load and display web content, such as a WebChat bot chat window, from directly within your app.

In addition, you'll be able to add user details (name and ID) from your CRM to a user's BotBonnie profile when they open a WebChat URL:

![](https://files.readme.io/9410bcf-linked-user-profile.png)

To open a WebChat bot in an app web view, complete the following steps:

  1. Create a WebChat bot
  2. Construct the WebChat URL
  3. (Optional) Add URL query parameters for CRM user details
  4. Load the WebChat URL in your app



* * *

## 

1\. Create a WebChat bot

Create a WebChat bot by going to **Channels** , then click the **WebChat** card. Refer to [this guide](doc:channels-webchat#set-up-webchat) for the detailed instruction.

![](https://files.readme.io/040d7deb45543d681908b023cdf25466897c1669f89d88cf73836eec4f48c3a9-WebChat_integration_1_1.png)

* * *

## 

2\. Construct the WebChat URL

After creating a WebChat bot, copy its page ID. The page ID is prefixed by `page-`.

![](https://files.readme.io/eff2a0138c2b2c38cb5e15e59a2703e7a5fcea4e469b02dc02f2a86d08d88b03-WebChat_integration_5.png)

Next, append the page ID to the web view's base URL as the `appId` query parameter (replace `<PAGE_ID>` with the page ID of the WebChat bot you'd like use):

WebChat URL format| Example page ID| Properly formatted URL  
---|---|---  
`https://chat.botbonnie.com/?standalone=true&appId=<PAGE_ID>`| `page-1234`| `https://chat.botbonnie.com/?standalone=true&appId=page-1234`  
  
* * *

## 

3\. (Optional) Add URL query parameters for CRM user details

To add CRM user details (name and user ID) to the user's BotBonnie profile when users open the WebChat URL, you'll need to dynamically generate the `bb_code` or `crmPayload` query parameter for the user and append it to the WebChat URL.

While `bb_code` and `crmPayload` both contain encrypted user details, they're generated in different ways, and they have different formats.

> 📘
> 
> Only include one of `bb_code` or `crmPayload` in the WebChat URL. Don't include both.

Account linking query parameter| Description| WebChat URL format with CRM user details  
---|---|---  
`bb_code`| (Recommended) Generate `bb_code` by encrypting user CRM details using the BotBonnie API.| `<https://chat.botbonnie.com/?standalone=true&appId=<PAGE_ID>&bb_code=<CODE>`  
`crmPayload`| Generate `crmPayload` by manually encrypting user CRM details.| `<https://chat.botbonnie.com/?standalone=true&appId=<PAGE_ID>&crmPayload=<CRM_PAYLOAD>`  
  
### 

(Recommended) Encrypting CRM data via BotBonnie API (`bb_code`)

  1. Retrieve your API token from the BotBonnie console.
  2. Send a `POST` request to `https://bb-webchat-api.appier.com/api/v2/server-api/auth-code` with the following headers and parameters:



#### 

Headers

Field| Value  
---|---  
`x-api-token`| Your BotBonnie API token.  
`content-type`| `application/json`  
  
#### 

Body parameters

Field| Type| Value  
---|---|---  
`pageId`| string| Required. The page ID of the WebChat bot. This must match the value used for `appId` in the WebChat URL.  
`crmId`| string| Required. The user's ID in your CRM.  
`crmName`| string| Optional. The user's name in your CRM.  
  
An example cURL request looks like this:

cURL
    
    
    curl -X POST 'https://bb-webchat-api.appier.com/api/v2/server-api/auth-code' \
    -H 'content-type: application/json' \
    -H 'x-api-token: <API_TOKEN>' \
    --data '{
    "crmId":"user_id_1234",
    "pageId":"page-1234",
    "crmName":"User name"
    }'

And the response will look like this, where `code` contains the user's encrypted CRM details:

JSON
    
    
    {
      "code": "crm-auth-xxxxxxxx"
    }

Append this value as the `bb_code` query parameter in the WebChat URL before loading it in the web view.

### 

Encrypt CRM data manually (`crmPayload`)

  1. Construct an object containing two keys: `crmId` and `crmName`:



JSON
    
    
    {
      "crmId": "...", // The user's ID in your CRM
      "crmName": "..." // The user's name in your CRM
    }

  2. Use SHA-256 to generate a 64-digit hash of your API secret. Your API secret can be retrieved from the BotBonnie console under **Settings > Advanced**.

![](https://files.readme.io/a2eea32cb3b70ec14cae4ff9f1c007a7fd53ed74fd27bbb9432ecde26be2785e-Starting_a_Chat_in_a_Web_view_3.png)

  3. Use AES-256-CTR to encrypt the object containing CRM details.
     1. `key`: The first 32 digits of your hashed API secret
     2. `IV`: Digits 33-48 of your hashed API secret
     3. Settings:
        1. Input format: text
        2. Mode: CTR
        3. Padding: PKCS5Padding
        4. Output format: hex
  4. Use the final encrypted string as the value for the `crmPayload` query parameter in the WebChat URL before loading it in the web view.



* * *

## 

4\. Load the WebChat URL in your app

In your app, load the properly constructed WebChat URL using the web view class methods corresponding to the platform you're developing for.

> 👍
> 
> ### 
> 
> Tip
> 
> To learn more about how to load a URL in a web view, refer to the developer guides provided for [iOS](https://developer.apple.com/design/human-interface-guidelines/components/content/web-views/) and [Android](https://developer.android.com/develop/ui/views/layout/webapps/webview).

If the WebChat bot's URL was constructed correctly and the URL was properly loaded by your app, users will be able to open a web view within your app to start a chat with the WebChat bot associated with the page ID you selected.
