# WhatsApp

> 📘
> 
> ### 
> 
> Premium feature
> 
> Contact your customer success manager to learn more about this feature.

You can integrate your WhatsApp Business Account with your BotBonnie account to start automating customer services and broadcasting engaging content to your users. Currently, WhatsApp supports two types of messages:

  * Business-initiated messages: Can only be sent to users who have opted in and must use the [**Message template**](doc:channels-whatsapp-manage-message-templates) created in Meta Business Manager and approved by Meta.
  * User-initiated messages: If a user initiates a chat and you reply within 24 hours, your replies can be free-format without requiring pre-approved templates. After 24 hours, replies are considered business-initiated messages and require pre-approved templates.



* * *

## 

Setup requirements

  1. [Create a Meta Business account](https://www.facebook.com/business/help/1710077379203657?id=180505742745347) if you don't already have one. Make sure you have admin access to your Meta Business account.
  2. Prepare a phone number for your WhatsApp Business Account:
     * If you already have a WhatsApp Business Account connected to another provider, fill out this [form](https://docs.google.com/forms/d/e/1FAIpQLScgd_70zC6OshJ7R0MseG1Q4Xkqs83BN8USzvoqotCRMkQhjQ/viewform) before proceeding.
     * Use a business phone number that isn't registered to WhatsApp yet or [rent a new number from Vonage](https://www.vonage.com/communications-apis/voice/pricing/).



> 📘
> 
> ### 
> 
> Note
> 
> Your chosen number must meet these requirements:
> 
>   * Can receive SMS from the United States.
>   * Isn't already registered to a WhatsApp account.
> 


  3. Contact your customer success manager with your Meta business account details and phone number. They'll connect your WhatsApp Business Account through Vonage and complete the connection with BotBonnie.
  4. Once your account connects successfully, you'll see a **Connected** status with a green check icon on both the **Connected accounts** list and the **Channels** page.

![](https://files.readme.io/37c3f90c8eada5b9f5b58627243902bbf9020aa8bae03e38fedc76fa37cfc04d-whatsapp-connected.png)

* * *

## 

Test connection

To test your connection, you can create an [AI rule](doc:ai-rules), then send a message containing the keyword you specified to your WhatsApp account. If your WhatsApp account was successfully connected, you'll receive the reply module you selected.

![](https://files.readme.io/6b45abf2d8348768d706f496c20dedf0ac54e6a214e2167a003c6bc94f300359-whatsapp-test-connection.png)

Once your connection is working, you can start engaging with your users. To send a business-initiated message using the [broadcast](doc:broadcasting-create-broadcasts) feature, follow the instructions to [create a message template](doc:channels-whatsapp-manage-message-templates).

__Updated 4 months ago

* * *

# Manage Message Templates

Once you've [connected your WhatsApp account with BotBonnie](doc:channels-whatsapp), you'll need to create message templates in [Meta Business Manager](https://business.facebook.com) to send business-initiated messages to users who have opted in to receive notifications.

* * *

## 

1\. Create a template in Meta Business Manager

Start by [creating a template](https://developers.facebook.com/docs/whatsapp/business-management-api/message-templates) in Meta Business Manager. Meta will review and approve your template before you can use it to send messages.

> 📘
> 
> ### 
> 
> Note
> 
> If you currently use WhatsApp Business with Meta's console directly, you'll need to migrate to a Vonage-managed account to integrate with BotBonnie. Contact your customer success manager before creating new templates.

### 

Configuring buttons in Meta Business Manager

To enable click tracking, you must follow these requirements when configuring buttons in Meta Business Manager:

  * **Type of action** : **Visit website**
  * **URL type** : **Dynamic**
  * **Website URL** : `https://r.botbonnie.com/`



Finally, under **Add sample URL** paste the following sample URL:
    
    
    https://r.botbonnie.com/S5O4i/934921be8361ca6dfe857e8abc152e04efd79a4526445331a6fa1d9ead7b4abe16a417b702dcdeffe8bf4763f8b747ee193f652e2030ed053b6e1744bf26788f36345b469ffb82cfe1d04d3ea8f053631d003aaebe168843dd0279edf44e63b5f4512cac762128701561c9e0c17cbf0c6e6d39f1912394329054573c3ba3222d558b7a0219d4a621b7891a274498

Important notes:

  * Button names cannot be modified after being set in Meta.
  * Maximum of two **Visit website** buttons per template.



* * *

## 

2\. Set up the template in BotBonnie

After you receive template approval from Meta, you can configure the template in BotBonnie. Go to **Channels** > **WhatsApp**. In the **Connected accounts** list, click **Manage Templates** for the account connected with Meta Business Manager, then click **\+ Create template**.

![](https://files.readme.io/03f51392d651aa4eb1ca0358247c2353679aef9813d32a258ddfc1db1860166f-whatsapp-mtm-create-template.png)

> 📘
> 
> ### 
> 
> Note
> 
> You must enter the exact same information and content in BotBonnie as in Meta Business Manager to ensure successful message delivery.

  1. Set up your **Template info** (the name and language must match Meta exactly).

![](https://files.readme.io/ca518f13a4660ed725f8e970b5a44b3898d5749668f5355bf737be094235ba8b-whatsapp-bb-template-info.png)

  2. In the **Content mapping** section, configure the **Header** mapping using one of the following options:
     1. **No need to map** : Select if you have a plain text header or no header at all.
     2. **Variable mapping** : Select if your header contains variables.
        1. **Fixed text** : Map variables to plain text content.
        2. **BotBonnie parameter** : Map variables to [parameters](doc:user-properties#parameters) like usernames.
     3. **Media mapping** : To map an image, a video, or a document. File size limits:
        1. Image: Up to 5 MB
        2. Video: Up to 20 MB
        3. Document: Up to 5 MB
  3. Configure the **Body** mapping using one of the following options:
     1. **No need to map** : Select if your body contains only plain text with no variables.
     2. **Variable mapping** : Select if your body contains variables.
        1. **Fixed text** : Map variables to plain text content.
        2. **BotBonnie parameters** : Map variables to personalized content. You can set up to three [parameters](doc:user-properties#parameters).
  4. Configure the **Button** mapping using one of the following options:
     1. **No need to map** : Select if no buttons are used in your Meta's message template.
     2. **Visit website** : Select if you added buttons in your Meta Business Manager. Enter the actual URL where you want users to go when they click the button.
        1. (Optional) Click variable's icon to modify your URL.
        2. (Optional) Set up [triggered actions](doc:triggered-actions) when users click the button.

# Choose a Journey Creation Method

Go to **Journey maps** and click **\+ Create journey map** to choose a journey creation method.

![](https://files.readme.io/3ceb2fe0d5eae98ba9ac88d96ee2f988d8fea48a0345988cc4942f88959e2d3f-create-journey-map.png)

After adding a new journey map from scratch or from a template, you can also your AI-powered assistant [journey copilot](doc:journey-copilot) to automatically generate a journey map based on a text description of the desired user journey.

![](https://files.readme.io/fe2aa3a-jm-copilot-prompt.png)

* * *

## 

1\. Choose a creation method

  * **Start from scratch**: Start with a blank canvas and manually add nodes to construct a user journey.
  * **Choose a template**: Start with a prebuilt template based on a specific use case, such as "New User Registration" or "First Purchase incentive, then modify the specifics to suit your needs.



### 

Start from scratch

To start with a blank canvas, click **\+ Start from scratch**.

![](https://files.readme.io/0adfba9-jm-start-from-scratch.png)

### 

Choose a template

The journey maps feature comes with a list of prebuilt templates based on specific use cases.

![](https://files.readme.io/2517c13-jm-choose-a-template.png)

Hover over a template to read a description of the journey and preview it by clicking the eye icon. If you're ready to start editing with the template, click **Use this template**.

![](https://files.readme.io/d99c3ea-jm-template-actions.png)

* * *

## 

2\. Configure basic settings

Fill out the basic settings of the journey and click **Continue**.

  * **Journey map name** : Enter a name for the journey map.
  * **Conversion event** : Add up to six events to track as conversions. Users are exited from the journey after completing a conversion event.
  * **Exit rule** : Add up to six exit events. Users are exited from the journey after completing the exit event.

![](https://files.readme.io/bafeddce467e1fb8ea712da033fde91b5af7e30e34a254fc4fbc98818fed2792-conversions-and-exit-rule.png)

> 📘
> 
> ### 
> 
> Exiting the journey
> 
> Users exit the journey in one of three ways:
> 
>   1. They reached an exit node on the map.
>   2. They completed the conversion event.
>   3. They're dropped because they're not reachable.
> 


Next, proceed to [add nodes](doc:add-journey-nodes) to your journey.

  


__Updated 7 months ago

* * *

[Create a Journey Map](/docs/creating-a-journey-map)[Journey Copilot](/docs/journey-copilot)

# Condition

The condition kit is designed to enhance user engagement and interaction through personalized experiences. Using the condition kit, you can specify different responses based on a user's tags or parameters, making interactions more dynamic and tailored. Condition kits can be used for:

  * **Channel-specific welcome messages** : Send different welcome messages depending on which channel the user is on.
  * **Action-based rewards** : For example, you can create a kit that gives a reward to users who have successfully linked their social account or completed a chatbot survey for the first time.
  * **Personalized marketing campaigns and recommendations** : Tailor messages or deliver specific product recommendations based on user behavior and interests. Offer exclusive discounts to frequent buyers or notify browsers about ongoing sales, ensuring targeted and relevant communication.



* * *

## 

Using condition kits

In the following guide, we'll create a condition kit that rewards new customers who have completed a survey with a prize code.

### 

1\. Add the condition kit

To add a condition kit to your flow, click **Rule kits** , then select **Conditions**.

![](https://files.readme.io/25ccf53-add-condition-kit.png)

### 

2\. Add condition-based scenarios

Click the conditions module you just added, then click **+Scenario**.

> 📘
> 
> ### 
> 
> Note: Multiple scenarios
> 
> The order in which the scenarios are created is also the priority they're evaluated in. If the conditions of multiple scenarios are met at the same time, the scenario that was created first will be used.

![](https://files.readme.io/63cf1ba-add-scenario.png)

Under the new scenario, go to **Rules** , then click on the condition to configure its settings. For example, you can add a condition that specifies users with the "Survey completed" tag should be sent the module containing the prize code.

![](https://files.readme.io/0bfd5a2-add-condition.png)

#### 

Add multiple conditions

To add additional conditions, click **Add condition**. You can specify up to 5 conditions per scenario. Note that the scenario will only be used if all the conditions are met.

### 

3\. Add a default scenario

The default scenario specifies which module should be sent if the user doesn't satisfy the conditions for any other scenario.

In this example, the default scenario consists of all users who haven't completed the survey, i.e. users missing the "Survey completed" tag will receive a message prompting them to take the survey.

![](https://files.readme.io/6ddae67-default-scenario.png)

__Updated 6 months ago

# Daily Check-In

> 👍
> 
> ### 
> 
> Supported channels
> 
> The daily check-in kit is available for the following channels:
> 
>   * Facebook
>   * Instagram
>   * LINE
>   * WebChat
> 


The daily check-in kit allows you to provide incentives for users to check in. You can set up consecutive or nonconsecutive check-ins, with different rewards for each check-in day or after completing a series of check-ins.

Using the daily check-in kit, you can easily:

  * Create campaigns with consecutive or nonconsecutive check-in types.
  * Set different rewards for each check-in milestone.
  * Track check-in progress and monitor remaining rewards.



### 

Example use case: Encouraging daily visits

A breakfast chain wants to increase customer visits to their physical stores. To accomplish this, the business can use BotBonnie's daily check-in kit with their LINE official channel's rich menu to offer rewards for daily visits. When customers complete seven consecutive daily check-ins, they can redeem a free cookie in-store.

![](https://files.readme.io/f3b776e-Screen_Shot_2023-05-29_at_4.59.11_PM.png)

* * *

## 

Daily check-in kit setup

### 

1\. Add the kit to your flow

From the left menu, go to **Flow** , then click **Advanced kits** and select **Daily check-in**.

![](https://files.readme.io/05e59cf7f1873525256531183e4606f9d50f852773a84cffe839c4c58cf7a4ba-daily-check-in-kit.png)

Click **Start editing** or double-click the kit to enter the kit's subflow.

![](https://files.readme.io/75e050c-check-in-start-editing.png)

Click on the **Check in** module to set up the kit.

![](https://files.readme.io/672364b-daily-check-in-settings.png)

### 

2\. Configure basic kit settings

Under the **Basic** section, complete the following settings:

  * **Kit name** : Enter a name to help you identify the kit in your flow.
  * **Campaign period** : Set a campaign start and end time.
  * **Headline text** : Enter the headline displayed on the check-in page.
  * **Terms and conditions** : Modify the terms and conditions of this campaign or type any information about the campaign to suit your needs. These terms and conditions are displayed at the bottom of the campaign page.

![](https://files.readme.io/d7b743f-small-Screen_Shot_2023-05-09_at_10.45.55_AM.png)

### 

3\. Set the check-in rules and rewards

In the **Rules** section, configure the check-in rules and day-specific reward rules to customize the check-in kit.

#### 

Check-in rules

Define the following rules:

Rule| Description| Example  
---|---|---  
Check-in type| Identify user eligibility for rewards based on consecutive or nonconsecutive check-in patterns.   
Options:   
• Consecutive   
• Nonconsecutive| • For three consecutive check-ins:   
If a user misses a day after two check-ins, the check-in count is reset, and the user has to start over with the consecutive check-ins.   
• For three nonconsecutive check-ins:   
Users can check in on any three days during the campaign.  
Maximum consecutive check-ins| Set a limit for consecutive check-ins (up to seven days).| If set to three days, users must check in three days in a row without missing any.  
Nonconsecutive check-ins| Set the number of nonconsecutive check-ins required (up to seven days).| If set to three days, users can check in on any three days within the campaign period, no matter the order.  
Maximum check-in rounds| Set how many times a user can complete the check-in process.   
Options:   
• Unlimited   
• Enter a number between 1 and 10.| If the maximum check-in round is set to 1, each user can complete the process only once during the campaign.  
  
![](https://files.readme.io/0fd43d552ac6086cfc46b850ba09d069388aeac727e24475563b3f5047ee817e-Frame_1.png)

> 📘
> 
> ### 
> 
> Note
> 
> When switching from nonconsecutive to consecutive check-ins, only check-ins from the previous day will carry forward.
> 
> For example, if the check-in campaign was configured as follows:
> 
>   * Start date: December 20
>   * Required check-ins: 5 days
>   * Change date: December 23
> 

> 
> After the switch:
> 
>   * System keeps: Only December 22 check-ins
>   * System resets: All other check-ins
>   * New check-ins: Start fresh consecutive count from December 23
> 


#### 

Day-specific reward configuration

For each check-in day, use the **Grant a reward** toggle to specify whether users receive a reward. For example, you can set no reward for the first two days and only provide a reward after the third check-in, whether consecutive or nonconsecutive.

![](https://files.readme.io/0fd43d552ac6086cfc46b850ba09d069388aeac727e24475563b3f5047ee817e-Frame_1.png)

If you've enabled **Grant a reward** , configure the following reward settings.

  * **Name:** Enter the name of the reward, for example, "20% off coupon". This name will be displayed to the user when they open the check-in page.
  * **Format:** Select a reward format. The following table describes the format in this kit.



Prize format| Description  
---|---  
**Inform users of prizes won**|  Users are immediately notified of their winning status. This prize type is ideal for digital prizes that don't require a physical exchange to occur.   
For example, this prize type can be used for digital assets such as images.   
![](https://files.readme.io/0894be5-Screen_Shot_2022-10-03_at_11.52.57_AM.png)  
**Physical exchange**|  This prize type is suitable for brick-and-mortar locations and physical events, where winning users can physically redeem a prize by presenting the winning screen to an employee.   
![](https://files.readme.io/a1d9b32-Screen_Shot_2022-10-03_at_12.02.12_PM.png)  
**Unique serial number**|  Upload a CSV file containing a list of unique serial numbers (text, barcode, or URL) that users can exchange for a prize. If this prize type is selected, winners will see the serial number when they win. For example:   
• **Text** : Users who win this prize will be assigned a unique serial number from the CSV file in sequential order. Serial numbers are suitable for coupons in e-commerce shops or rewards in mobile games.   
• **Barcode** : The serial number will be converted and displayed as a barcode. Barcodes are suitable for physical stores with POS machines.   
• **URL** : The CSV file should contain a list of URLs. When users click the button, they will be directed to the URL to receive the reward. URLs are suitable for distributing LINE points, as it allows users to redeem points with a single click.   
![](https://files.readme.io/08193c1-Screen_Shot_2022-10-03_at_1.59.22_PM.png)Please note the following serial number file requirements:   
• Only CSV files are supported   
• The file must be less than 10 MB   
• The file must contain a single column with the list of serial numbers   
![](https://files.readme.io/8b60599-Screen_Shot_2022-10-03_at_2.03.18_PM.png)  
**Point card**|  Select an existing point card kit to send to users who win.  
  
  * **Quantity:** Set the number of rewards available. This setting is unavailable for **Unique serial number** since the number of rewards available will be based on the number of serial numbers in the CSV file.
  * **Image:** Upload the main reward image if needed. This image will be shown on the reward result page.



### 

4\. Check the messaging platform status

In the **Others** section, verify that all messaging platforms are properly configured and active.

![](https://files.readme.io/c19b33e-small-Screen_Shot_2023-05-10_at_6.08.20_PM.png)

* * *

## 

Check-in status

After you start running the kit, you can go to the **Check-in Status** section to see the number of users who have checked in for each day and monitor the number of rewards left in the **Remaining** column.

![](https://files.readme.io/dfd9465af2e6c525dfd49d5d39028295a561d5e747c9654c238084102eec2eb7-check-in-status.png)

* * *

## 

Reset kit data

You can reset a kit if you've used it for internal testing and want to clear all test data before officially launching your campaign.

When you reset a kit, the following data will be permanently deleted:

  * Participant data: Includes user details, entries, and rewards.
  * Rewards: All distributed rewards will be returned to the reward pool.
  * Check-in status: All check-in progress will be reset.



> 🚧
> 
> ### 
> 
> Caution
> 
> Resetting the kit will permanently delete all participant data and check-in progress. This action cannot be undone.

### 

How to reset a kit

  1. Go to the **Reset** section.
  2. Click **Reset**.
  3. Enter "Reset" into the input box.
  4. Click **Reset** again to confirm your choice.

![](https://files.readme.io/95c055ee6d2b9887d35963a2dcea0618612bd95b2f9356b95c84d44cb677d968-reset-kit.png)

__Updated 6 months ago

* * *

# Dashboard

BotBonnie's dashboard feature allows you to see the user count and message count from your channels. To open the dashboard, go to **Analytics > Dashboard**.

![](https://files.readme.io/5176eef-nav-dashboard.png)

Start by selecting the date range.

![](https://files.readme.io/77763be-Screen_Shot_2023-10-12_at_10.53.05_AM.png)

The date range selected is applied to:

  * (A) User count and message count graphs
  * (B) New users, active users, broadcasted users, blocked users, total messages
  * (C) Popular modules

![](https://files.readme.io/bb7b5ae-Screen_Shot_2023-10-11_at_7.17.04_PM.png)

## 

User count and message count

The line graphs show the user count or message count from all channels and from each channel. You can click the channel type under the x-axis to hide or display the channel in the graph.

![](https://files.readme.io/f5d3e32-bb_dashboard.gif)

Under the **User count** tab, you can select to see the number of users for **New users** , **Active users** , or **Broadcasted users**.

![](https://files.readme.io/5adf4fa-Screen_Shot_2023-10-12_at_11.33.50_AM.png)

Under the **Message count** tab, the graph shows the number of messages from each channel. You can select to see the number of messages that are **From bot** or **From users**.

![](https://files.readme.io/9924bb0-Screen_Shot_2023-10-12_at_11.34.01_AM.png)

## 

Key metrics

The definitions of the key metrics are listed below. The percentage next to the count is the percent changed compared to the previous period.

![](https://files.readme.io/07681fc-Screen_Shot_2023-10-11_at_7.54.03_PM.png)

  * **Total users** : The number of all users who have interacted with the bot or have received messages from the bot before.
    * Users who have blocked your bot are not counted.
    * This number is not affected by the selected date range.
  * **New users** : The number of users whose first interaction with the bot or the first message received from the bot happened during the selected date range.
  * **Active users** : The number of users who have actively interacted with your bot during the selected date range.
    * Here are some examples of active interactions:
      * The user sends a message to the bot.
      * The user clicks on a button in the module.
      * The user comments under a post with the auto-reply feature.
      * The user adds the LINE Official Account as a friend.
    * Here are some examples of what does **not** count toward active interactions:
      * The user receives a message from the bot.
      * The user receives a comment reply from the bot.
      * The user reacts to bot messages with emojis.



> 📘
> 
> ### 
> 
> Note
> 
> Receiving a LINE targeted broadcast counts as an active interaction, while receiving other types of broadcast does not count toward active interactions.

  * **Broadcasted users** : The number of users who have received your broadcasts during the selected date range.
  * **Blocked users** : The number of users who blocked the LINE Official Account during the selected date range. This data is available for LINE only.
  * **Total messages** : The number of messages the bot sent to and received from users during the selected date range. Here's an example. In the image below, there are 3 messages total with each module or user input counting as 1 message.

![](https://files.readme.io/b07c595-Screen_Shot_2023-10-11_at_1.58.19_PM.png)

> 📘
> 
> ### 
> 
> Note
> 
> It's possible for the number of active users to exceed the number of total users. If users blocked your channels outside of the selected date range, these blocked users will be included in the active users count, but not in the total users count. This is because the selected date range is not applied to total users.

## 

User growth in 7 days

The line graph shows the changes in user count in the last 7 days. The user count here includes blocked users.

![](https://files.readme.io/92b5b8d-User_growth_in_7_days.png)

## 

Popular modules

Popular modules are the modules with the most number of messages sent during the date range selected.

If the module includes multiple messages, each message counts as a sent message. If you want to see the number of times the module is sent to users, you can divide the **Total sent** count by the number of messages in the module.

![](https://files.readme.io/64fdf54-popular_module.png)

## 

Exporting data

To export the data, click **Export**.

![](https://files.readme.io/3e9cfb3-Screen_Shot_2023-10-11_at_6.50.41_PM.png)

Select the date range to export, type your email address, and click **Confirm**.

![](https://files.readme.io/fcce589-Screen_Shot_2023-10-11_at_6.52.48_PM.png)

After the export is complete, you'll receive an email containing a link to download the dashboard report.

![](https://files.readme.io/3d4a543-dashboard-report.png)

__Updated 12 months ago

# Automatic Status Notifications

> 👍
> 
> ### 
> 
> Supported platforms
> 
>   * Cyberbiz
>   * SHOPLINE
>   * Shopify
> 


Automatic status notifications keep your users informed about order updates, loyalty program changes, and product availability across supported e-commerce platforms and messaging channels.

Once configured, the system automatically sends real-time notifications to users when specific events occur in their accounts. Users receive these updates through their preferred messaging channels after completing the account linking process. You can review the supported notification types, platforms, and channels or check the set up instructions.

![](https://files.readme.io/1fc44c9-automatic-status-notifications.png)

* * *

## 

Prerequisites

Before you configure automatic status notifications:

  1. Connect your [e-commerce platform](doc:e-commerce-integrations).
  2. Ensure users complete [account linking](doc:e-commerce-integration-account-linking).



* * *

## 

Supported notifications

The following table shows which notification types are available for each platform and messaging channel:

Category| Supported notification| Supported platform| Supported channel  
---|---|---|---  
Orders| • Order placed   
• Order shipped   
• Order delivered   
• Order refunded| • Cyberbiz   
• Shopify   
• SHOPLINE| • LINE   
• Messenger  
Points| • Points received   
• Points used| • Cyberbiz   
• SHOPLINE| • LINE  
Reward credits| • Reward credits received   
• Reward credits used| • SHOPLINE| • LINE  
Coupons| • Coupon received| • SHOPLINE| • LINE  
[Restock notifications](doc:e-commerce-features-restock-notifications)| • Subscribed to restock notifications   
• Product restocked| • Shopify| • LINE  
  
Note: Due to Meta's notification policy prohibiting messages containing promotional content, some automatic status notifications (such as those related to points or reward credits) are not supported on Messenger.

* * *

## 

Set up automatic status notifications

  1. Open the project launcher in the top navigation bar, select **Common settings** > **EC platforms** , and then select your connection.
  2. Navigate to the **Set up notifications** step. At the top, check the box to **Enable automatic status notifications**.
  3. Check to enable the specific notification type you want to activate. For example, select **Order placed**. You can modify the message content and switch the supported channels to preview how it will appear to users.
  4. After configuring your notifications, click **Save** in the upper-right corner to apply your changes.

![](https://files.readme.io/92eb643fb26c3a289e7f4bce1a710d12c4fa5a2434b5b9d6594aa2f5149ed7b9-shopify-notifications.gif)

__Updated 7 months ago

* * *

# Member Card

> 👍
> 
> ### 
> 
> Supported channels and platforms
> 
>   * LINE
>   * Cyberbiz
>   * Shopify
>   * SHOPLINE
> 


Use member cards to connect online and offline experiences by offering digital membership cards through LINE. Users can easily check their membership details—such as tier levels, points balance, and exclusive benefits—right from their accounts. It also enables you to offer seamless benefits across online and offline physical stores, strengthening customer relationships and supporting your Online-Merge-Offline (OMO) strategy.

![](https://files.readme.io/597d00ee79d796408fcb6b3fabd16d37ca4791f27455781613e8cf9a51aaca9b-member-card-image.png)

* * *

## 

Usage scenarios

The member card feature allows you to connect online and offline experiences seamlessly. Here are some common scenarios where users can interact with their digital member card via LINE:

__Checking membership details

Once the member card is integrated with LINE, users can access their membership details directly within the app, including:

  * Point balance
  * Real-time total spend
  * Order history (up to 90 days)
  * Discount coupons and redemption status



__Presenting the digital member card during offline purchases

When checking out in a physical store, users can present their digital member card via LINE instead of using a physical card. Store staff can easily scan the barcode through your existing SHOPLINE-compatible POS system to apply rewards or verify membership.

__Redeeming benefits and coupons

After linking their account, users can redeem offline coupons directly from their digital member card. This includes instant coupon application at the point of transaction, providing a seamless experience both online and in-store. Users can enjoy exclusive benefits and discounts, strengthening engagement with your brand.

* * *

## 

Prerequisites

Contact your customer success manager and submit two image files for the member card display:

  * Member card background image
  * Brand logo (for fallback coupon images)



The images must meet the following specifications:

Specification| Member card background image| Brand logo  
---|---|---  
**Supported formats**|  JPG, PNG| JPG, PNG  
**Recommended sizes**|  351 × 220 px (16:10 aspect ratio)| 72 × 72 px (1:1 aspect ratio)  
**Design guidelines**|  • **Logo placement** : Place the logo in a corner of the background image. Avoid the center area, which is reserved for the member’s profile photo. Recommended logo height: 20-28 px.   
• **Padding** : Leave at least 20 px of padding around the edges for visual balance.   
• **Contrast** : Ensure the logo stands out against the background.   
  
![](https://files.readme.io/d7d6f21b9a7ef9f604944b1c2d7f60056f55ee6d9d181034fe06b363aa7dc47d-member-card-specs.png)| • **Padding** : Leave at least 8 px of padding around the edges for visual balance.   
• **Contrast** : Ensure the logo remains visible against the background.   
  
![](https://files.readme.io/316d46640a9e815664654019fd2a38eee9e4819095cdbfb85420350d959d6861-brand-logo-specs.png)  
  
> 📘
> 
> ### 
> 
> Note
> 
> To ensure your member card and logo images are displayed clearly on high-DPI displays such as Retina screens, prepare images at multiple resolutions while maintaining the recommended aspect ratio.

* * *

## 

Set up a member card in LINE

After integration, you can add a member card via chatbot flow or the LINE menu.

### 

Via chatbot flow

  1. Go to **Flow** , [add a module](doc:modules-and-messages#adding-a-module) and click **\+ Add button** to add a new button.

![](https://files.readme.io/2a016eb232ed4775e26296806548fce24144f8145aa997b2fbb27b6c24774bc4-flow-add-member-card.png)

  2. In the button settings, open **On click** dropdown and select **Integrated EC service** > **Member card**.

![](https://files.readme.io/e9638165208c1542f73cce91f9f7d5a15b41d19e40c40526d751b06f688c1487-flow-membercard.png)

### 

Via LINE menu

  1. Go to **Content** > **Menus** and open the LINE menu tab to add a menu.

![](https://files.readme.io/a5946ee865fbe463a20c7b9de7d7f4dea147ff11a2472e885e22e6423be482fd-add-line-menu-member-card.png)

  2. Under **Image upload and layout settings** , select a button area. Then, select **Integrated EC service** > **Member card** from **Click the block** dropdown.

![](https://files.readme.io/f74a4da22cd189ef44112c18e7a8f2a2d562ec81ddf1b4d4fef8a4f08306794f-menu-membercard.png)

__Updated 4 months ago

# Product Tag Syncing

> 👍
> 
> ### 
> 
> Supported platform
> 
>   * Shopify
> 


Product tag syncing automatically adds product tags from a user’s purchases to their profile. These tags show users' interests and preferences based on their order history.

To enable product tag syncing, ensure that:

  1. [Your e-commerce platform is connected to BotBonnie](doc:e-commerce-integrations).
  2. [Users have linked their accounts to your store](doc:e-commerce-integration-account-linking).



> 📘
> 
> ### 
> 
> Note
> 
> This feature requires manual activation. Contact your customer support manager to enable product tag syncing after completing the steps above.

* * *

## 

How it works

The syncing process happens automatically in three steps:

  1. The order is placed: When a user with a linked account places an order on your store, the sync process triggers.
  2. Product tags are identified: BotBonnie detects the product tags that are associated with the purchased items.
  3. Tags are synced: Product tags are added to the user's profile and appear as **Synced tags** (yellow label).



🛒

Order placed:

User with linked account places order

→

🔍

Tags identified:

BotBonnie scans purchased items for tags

→

🏷️

Tags synced:

Tags added to user profile

> 📘
> 
> ### 
> 
> Note
> 
> Only product tags associated with the purchased items are synced. For more information on creating and using product tags, see [Shopify's guide](https://help.shopify.com/en/manual/shopify-admin/productivity-tools/using-tags).

* * *

## 

View and use synced tags

You can view synced tags in these locations:

  * Live chat
  * User properties



### 

Live chat

In [live chat](doc:live-chat#manage-user-details), synced tags appear in user profiles as yellow labels that give agents quick insights into users' purchase history.

![](https://files.readme.io/a153d38b168362bfdebeb94300a4a8d905aecb31688a85bc13d36f95f206f5ce-ec-product-tag-live-chat.png)

### 

User properties

In [**User properties**](doc:user-properties#tags), synced tags appear in the tag list with yellow labels under the **Synced tag** type. When you filter audiences or create [segments](doc:segments), these tags are shown with the marker **(Synced tag)** so you can easily identify and use them.

![](https://files.readme.io/22739ba4a88cbd9c97518f4df7806043ee883554f39e08502b46a0e2f919fba5-condition-filter-tag.png)

__Updated 7 months ago

# Query Transaction History

Once a user links their account, they can query their transaction history directly through the chatbot. To enable this feature, set up entry points in your chatbot flow, LINE menu, or Messenger menu. Additionally, agents can access a user's recent order history within the console's [live agent chat](doc:live-chat#managing-user-details) to better understand purchasing behavior.

![](https://files.readme.io/ed73e55-order-history.png)

* * *

## 

Prerequisites

Before you configure the entry point for querying transaction history:

  1. Connect your [e-commerce platform](doc:e-commerce-integrations).
  2. Ensure users complete [account linking](doc:e-commerce-integration-account-linking).



## 

Set up an entry point for querying transaction history

You can create multiple entry points to enable users to query their transaction history, either within a chatbot flow or directly from channel-specific menus.

Entry point| Configuration steps  
---|---  
Chatbot flow| 

  1. Go to **Flow** and [add a button](doc:button#adding-a-button) to a module.   

  2. Under **On click** , select **Integrated EC service** > **Query transaction history**.   
  
![](https://files.readme.io/24b2bb10c41d4e32d439f33f53d98f0a9974e3697f0769b4641897d2dde01766-flow-query-transaction-history.png)

  
[LINE menu](doc:menus#line-menu-setup)| 

  1. In your LINE menu configuration, go to the **Image upload and layout settings**.   

  2. Under **Click the block** , select **Integrated EC service** > **Query transaction history**.  
  


![](https://files.readme.io/0ee8568c6d5b1bd6944f6d4a9ef8846ee9933546d2fb81924c9b2c6730ea5136-query-transaction-history.gif)  
[Messenger menu](doc:menus#messenger-menu-setup)| 

  1. [Create a module](doc:modules-and-messages#adding-a-module) in your chatbot flow that includes the button for account linking.   

  2. In your Messenger menu configuration, go to the **Persistent menu item settings** , click **\+ Add an item** , and then select the flow module you created earlier.   
  


![](https://files.readme.io/d4d4ee188f2322802761c290d730c592235f5ce5cd18a210a418f96a353fd57c-messenger-module.png)

# Restock Notifications

> 📘
> 
> ### 
> 
> Note
> 
> The restock notification feature is only supported for [Shopify](doc:e-commerce-integration-shopify) integrations.

> 👍
> 
> ### 
> 
> Supported channel
> 
>   * LINE
> 


Users can subscribe to out-of-stock products and get alerts when they're restocked, which can help you attract more customers and increase revenue. To understand the user experience, see how the feature works, then follow these steps to configure restock notifications.

* * *

## 

How restock notification works

Users subscribe to out-of-stock products and receive notifications when products are restocked:

  1. Users subscribe to restock notifications
  2. Users receive notification when the product is restocked



### 

1\. Users subscribe to restock notifications

When a user views an out-of-stock product, they can select which variant they'd like to receive notifications for and click **Restock notification** with the LINE logo.

![](https://files.readme.io/f4eb4d83a3a4be44fd637b91f096789da3af723ec050a84ffd6d070cb7197c9a-EC-product_page.png)

Then, select the product variant you want to subscribe to and click the **Receive restock notification** button via LINE's green button.

![](https://files.readme.io/6313fd7d75986a342d18d278cfb08c024a50999bb60fca7f77bdc44c5d2941e2-LINE_subscription_button.png)

After selecting the variant:

  * Mobile: Users will be redirected to the LINE app to complete the subscription and receive a confirmation message.
  * Desktop: Users will see a QR code, which they need to scan with the LINE app to finish the subscription and receive the same confirmation.

![](https://files.readme.io/11597d9a2d518154fabcf8a030f9a00ee562f946b4828c2de06c218533be9762-mobile.png) ![](https://files.readme.io/99efd588e94fba6a8f96226f7cc4fad1ca0050c01ab3bced6a79208126bd314d-Frame_1.png)

### 

2\. Users receive notification when the product is restocked

When the product is back in stock, the user will receive a LINE notification, displaying the product image, variant details, and a direct link to your Shopify store, making it easy for them to complete their purchase.

![](https://files.readme.io/6d038225e1571e22520c330a5b1eb18571b34e5a55116ce51e0d164605b832dc-restocked.png)

* * *

## 

Configure restock notifications

Follow these instructions to configure restock notifications for your Shopify store.

  1. Enable restock notifications
  2. Update your Shopify store's custom Liquid code



### 

1\. Enable restock notifications

  1. Enable the [automatic status notifications](doc:e-commerce-features-automatic-status-notifications#set-up-automatic-status-notifications).
  2. Under the **Restock notification** section, select both **Subscribe to restock notifications** and **Product restocked**.

![](https://files.readme.io/99744a777e84abdef527371a625fcb8909ba474d80dd8f770f2be9ec16b0cbfe-subscribe-to-restock-notifications.png)

  3. Create and customize the message content for both notifications and preview how it will appear to users.

![](https://files.readme.io/b35ba1214fe02b4a510b82c356760b709cc9593f846feb104f32185d773133f9-restock_message.png)

  4. After selecting and modifying the type of notifications you want to send, click **Save**.



### 

2\. Update your Shopify store's custom Liquid code

  1. In the BotBonnie console, under **Restock notifications** , expand the **LIFF app URL** to copy the URL.

![](https://files.readme.io/99d2d89b0d1a9bd27530dea875e937f06f2f2f54784db15d3eb281950b6c968e-LIFF_app_url.png)
  2. Log in to your Shopify account and go to the **Home page** , then **Products** , select **Default product** , and navigate to the **Template**.

  3. Add a **Custom liquid** section between the **Product page** and **Product content**.

![](https://files.readme.io/08b51b73564aa83daa181a3bf86da001a4a99ac1d3361909f6f7959531a40778-Add_custom_liquid_section.png)
  4. Modify the **Custom liquid** section by pasting the code snippet below into the **Liquid** field.


![](https://files.readme.io/cac1a45034e5a654e3b2fac79c22c8f1de1d31e86039f13e3daf725e6f382b98-Edit_code_snippet_and_URL.png)

> 🚧
> 
> ### 
> 
> Warning
> 
> Make sure to replace the placeholder LIFF app URL in the code below with your actual LIFF app URL from the BotBonnie console. This step is required for restock notifications to work properly.

HTML
    
    
    <!-- Start to copy -->
    <script>
      // REPLACE YOUR URL FORM CONSOLE HERE
      window.BB_RESTOCK_LIFF_BASE_URL = 'https://liff.line.me/YOUR_LIFF_ID_AND_PARAMS';
    
      // ADD YOUR CUSTOMIZE BUTTON STYLE HERE
      const customizeStyle = document.createElement('style');
      customizeStyle.innerHTML = `
        .bb-restock-button {
          // Add your custom styles here
          display: flex;
          align-items: center;
          justify-content: center;
          height: 40px;
          width: 100%;
          background-color: #06c755;
          color: white;
          margin: 0 auto;
          border-radius: 0;
          cursor: pointer;
          font-size: 16px;
          text-align: center;
          border: none; 
        }
        .bb-restock-button:hover {
          // Add your custom hover styles here
        }
      `;
      document.head.appendChild(customizeStyle);
    </script>
    
    <script>
      window.variants = {
        {% for variant in product.variants %}
        "{{ variant.id }}": {
          id: "{{ variant.id }}",
          title: "{{ variant.title }}",
          image_url: {% if variant.image != blank %}"{{ variant | image_url: width: 450 }}"{% else %} "{{ product | image_url: width: 450 }}" {% endif %},
          inventory_quantity: "{{ variant.inventory_quantity }}",
        },
        {% endfor %}
      };
    
      window.product = {
        id: "{{ product.id }}",
        title: "{{ product.title }}",
        featured_image: "{{ product | image_url: width: 450 }}"
      };
    </script>
    
    <style>
      #bb-restock-modal {
        display: none;
        position: fixed;
        top: 0;
        left: 0;
        right: 0;
        bottom: 0;
        width: 100%;
        height: 100%;
        background: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.6);
        justify-content: center;
        align-items: center;
        opacity: 0;
        transition: opacity 0.5s ease;
        z-index: 100000000000000001;
      }
    
      #bb-restock-modal.show {
        opacity: 1;
      }
    
      .bb-restock-modal-content {
        position: relative;
        width: 100%;
        max-width: 490px;
        padding: 20px 16px;
        border-radius: 4px;
        text-align: left;
        color: black;
        background: white;
      }
    
      .bb-restock-modal-content-footer {
        width: 100%;
        display: flex;
        align-items: center;
        justify-content: center;
      }
    
      .bb-restock-modal-close-button {
        position: absolute;
        top: 12px;
        right: 12px;
        color: #ccc;
        background: none;
        border: none;
        padding: 5px;
        cursor: pointer;
        font-size: 16px;
      }
    
      .bb-restock-modal-close-button:hover {
        color: #000;
      }
    
      .bb-restock-modal-header {
        font-size: 16px;
        font-weight: 400;
        margin-bottom: 12px;
      }
    
      .bb-restock-modal-title {
        font-size: 16px;
        font-weight: 700;
        margin-bottom: 12px;
      }
    
      .bb-restock-modal-select-container {
        margin-bottom: 12px;
      }
    
      .bb-restock-modal-select {
        padding: 0 8px;
      }
    
      #bb-restock-modal-variant-select {
        width: 100%;
        height: 40px;
        border: solid 1px rgb(229, 229, 229);
      }
    
      .bb-subscribe-restock-button {
        height: 40px;
        width: 360px;
        max-width: 100%;
        padding: 8px 16px;
        background-color: #06c755;
        text-align: center;
        color: white;
        cursor: pointer;
        border: none;
      }
      .bb-restock-button-container {
        width: 100%;
      }
    </style>
    
    <div class="bb-restock-button-container">
      <button class="bb-restock-button" role="button" id="BBRestockButton">
        Receive LINE restock notification
      </button>
    </div>
    
    <template id="bb-restock-modal-template">
      <div id="bb-restock-modal">
        <div class="bb-restock-modal-content">
          <button class="bb-restock-modal-close-button" id="closeModalButton">x</button>
          <div class="bb-restock-modal-header" id="modalHeaderText"></div>
          <div class="bb-restock-modal-title" id="modalProductTitle"></div>
          <div class="bb-restock-modal-select-container">
            <select class="bb-restock-modal-select" id="bb-restock-modal-variant-select"></select>
          </div>
          <div class="bb-restock-modal-content-footer">
            <button class="bb-subscribe-restock-button" id="submitModalButton">
              Receive LINE restock notifications
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </template>
    
    <script>
      const translations = {
        en: {
          header: 'Restock notifications',
          buttonText: 'Receive LINE restock notifications',
        },
        ja: {
          header: '再入荷の通知',
          buttonText: 'LINEで再入荷通知を受け取る',
        },
        zh: {
          header: '補貨通知',
          buttonText: '接收 LINE 補貨通知',
        },
        ko: {
          header: '재입고 알림',
          buttonText: 'LINE 재입고 알림 받기',
        },
      };
    
      function getBrowserLanguage() {
        let lang = navigator.language || navigator.userLanguage;
        if (lang?.includes('ja')) return 'ja';
        else if (lang?.includes('ko')) return 'ko';
        else if (lang?.includes('zh')) return 'zh';
        else return 'en';
      }
    
      function openModal(e) {
        e.preventDefault();
        const restockModal = document.querySelector('#bb-restock-modal');
        restockModal.style.display = 'flex';
        setTimeout(function () {
          restockModal.classList.add('show');
        }, 10);
      }
    
      function closeModal() {
        const restockModal = document.querySelector('#bb-restock-modal');
        restockModal.classList.remove('show');
        setTimeout(function () {
          restockModal.style.display = 'none';
        }, 500);
      }
    
      function subscribeRestock(e) {
        e.preventDefault();
        const variantId = document.querySelector('#bb-restock-modal-variant-select')?.value;
        if (!variantId) {
          return closeModal();
        }
        const variant = window.variants?.[variantId] || {};
        const variantTitle = variant?.title;
        const variantImage = variant?.image_url;
        const productId = window?.product?.id;
        const productTitle = window?.product?.title;
    
        const url = new URL(window?.BB_RESTOCK_LIFF_BASE_URL);
        url.searchParams.append('variantId', variantId);
        url.searchParams.append('variantTitle', variantTitle);
        url.searchParams.append('productId', productId);
        url.searchParams.append('productTitle', productTitle);
        url.searchParams.append('productFeaturedImage', variantImage);
        console.log('url', url);
        window.open(url, '_blank', 'noopener,noreferrer');
        closeModal();
      }
    
      function init() {
        const lang = getBrowserLanguage();
        const translation = translations[lang] || translations['en'];
    
        const template = document.querySelector('#bb-restock-modal-template').content.cloneNode(true);
        const body = document.querySelector('body');
        body.appendChild(template);
    
        const restockButton = document.querySelector('#BBRestockButton');
        restockButton.innerText = translation.buttonText;
        restockButton.addEventListener('click', openModal);
    
        const restockModal = document.querySelector('#bb-restock-modal');
        restockModal.querySelector('#modalHeaderText').innerText = translation.header;
        restockModal.querySelector('#modalProductTitle').innerText = window?.product?.title;
    
        const selectElement = restockModal.querySelector('#bb-restock-modal-variant-select');
        Object.keys(window?.variants || {}).forEach((variantId) => {
          const variant = window.variants[variantId];
          if (Number(variant.inventory_quantity) === 0) {
            const option = document.createElement('option');
            option.value = variant.id;
            option.dataset.title = variant.title;
            option.dataset.inventory = variant.inventory_quantity;
            option.textContent = variant.title;
            selectElement.appendChild(option);
          }
        });
        restockModal.querySelector('#submitModalButton').innerText = translation.buttonText;
        restockModal.querySelector('#closeModalButton').addEventListener('click', closeModal);
        restockModal.querySelector('#submitModalButton').addEventListener('click', subscribeRestock);
        restockModal.addEventListener('click', function (event) {
          if (event.target === restockModal) closeModal();
        });
      }
    
      init();
    </script>
    
    <!-- End to copy -->

  5. Replace the value of `liffUrl` with the LIFF app URL you copied from the BotBonnie console.
  6. Remove unnecessary text and **Subheading** from the **Custom liquid** section.

![](https://files.readme.io/28714440a8c3c89fe13a5b281dd81179ebbbc557f3f2739f51376cb0f4042410-Remove_unnecessary_section.png)

  7. Save the changes.



After enabling restock notifications, you can monitor performance metrics in the analytics dashboard. For more information, see [Restock notification metrics](doc:e-commerce-insights#shopify-restock-notification-summary).

# E-commerce Insights

> 📘
> 
> ### 
> 
> Premium feature
> 
> E-commerce insights is a premium feature. Contact your customer success manager to learn more.

E-commerce insights allow you to track key customer engagement and sales metrics from your connected e-commerce platform. The supported platform and analytics are as follows:

Platform| Insights  
---|---  
Shopify| Restock notification summary  
  
* * *

## 

Viewing e-commerce insights

  1. Go to **Analytics** > **E-commerce insights**.

![](https://files.readme.io/ff78d19dbb8052039999f88cc8b172d3b93380b4ebf11b41fb4f2f830a065edd-e-commerce-insights.png)

  2. In the upper right, use the date selector to choose a custom date range, or select one of the preset time ranges (**Last 7 days** , **Last 14 days** , **Last 30 days** , or **Last 90 days**) from the quick filter menu.

![](https://files.readme.io/58c8a6589cc9e0435133ef135a93ceaa9e05159b3b503ad9405b138f367d6868-custom-date-range.png)

* * *

## 

Shopify restock notification summary

The Shopify restock notification summary presents three key metrics: subscriptions, notifications, and purchases.

### 

Prerequisites

Before using restock notification summary:

  1. [Connect your Shopify account to BotBonnie](doc:e-commerce-integration-shopify).
  2. [Enable restock notifications](doc:e-commerce-features-restock-notifications).
  3. Set up [customer account linking](doc:e-commerce-integration-account-linking) to enable purchase tracking. Without account linking, purchase metrics won't be recorded.



### 

Understanding key metrics

The dashboard displays restock notification metrics including:

Metric| Description  
---|---  
Subscriptions| The number of times customers subscribed to restock notifications for out-of-stock products. Each product subscription is counted once, regardless of how many variants (such as different sizes, colors, or styles) of that product exist. For example, if a product has three color variants, a subscription to this product counts as one subscription.  
Notifications| The number of restock notifications sent to subscribed customers when a product or its variant is restocked. Each notification sent counts as one.  
Purchases| The number of products purchased by customers after receiving restock notifications. Each product with an active restock notification subscription counts as one purchase, even when multiple products are purchased in the same order. Only purchases from users who have completed the account linking process are counted, and the purchase must occur after the customer receives the notification.  
  
Each metric is shown in two formats:

  * Count: The total number of events for each metric.
  * Percentage: The share of customers progressing from the previous stage. For example, in the summary below, 38.2% of subscribers received a notification, and 0% of those who received one made a purchase.

![](https://files.readme.io/e1a86713c406e842250f59454fe0cb2c37e95d746d1d05a4617fd4dc2aa6da5d-shopify-restock-summary.png)

__Updated 7 months ago

# 91APP

> 👍
> 
> ### 
> 
> Supported channel
> 
>   * LINE
> 


Connect your 91APP store to the BotBonnie chatbot to enable users who link their accounts to view their shopping information.

Follow these setup steps to add a connection:

  1. Obtain credentials
  2. Connect with 91APP
  3. (Optional) Set up account linking



After setup, you can [manage connections](doc:e-commerce-integrations#manage-connections) on whenever necessary.

* * *

## 

1\. Obtain credentials

To connect BotBonnie to the 91APP platform, contact the 91APP support team to request the following credentials:

  * API key (`x-api-key`)
  * Webhook shared secret (which includes a username and secret)



* * *

## 

2\. Connect with 91APP

Follow these steps to connect your 91APP store to BotBonnie.

  1. Open the project launcher in the top navigation bar, select **Common settings** , and then click **EC platforms** in the left navigation panel.

![](https://files.readme.io/42d9e57b124e8eaa22c136bae1bc157fed1c1f4b734c857cb4a8a5c4c63055d4-nav-ec-platforms.png)

  2. Click the **91APP** card to start the connection setup.

![](https://files.readme.io/2a4487d2322bcd7765055a1e9f112eab8dc5beb185e2bb998900e4d8926100a8-91-app-card.png)

  3. Enter a name for your connection.

![](https://files.readme.io/dc696a97ee85b97c370742deac55c66ad73061d5cd1b3df72a0afb69cbec42d2-connection-name.png)

  4. If you haven't connected a LINE Official Account in 91APP, expand the **Connect your LINE Official Account in 91APP Marketing Cloud** section and follow the provided guides to complete the setup.
  5. Click **\+ Choose page(s)** to select the page you want to connect.

![](https://files.readme.io/484b0f23d43c10df7dfc9539d3acd96e4435cb91fae05216dbab07aa7e76ce64-choose-page-line.png)

  6. Input your account details in both your BotBonnie console and your 91APP store settings.



Configuration| Source| Destination| Instruction  
---|---|---|---  
**1\. Input API key and webhook share secret**|  Email from 91APP| BotBonnie console| Enter your **Shop serial number** , **x-api-key** , and **Webhook share secret** into corresponding fields.   
  
![](https://files.readme.io/0b73e9527029dfd70ee6634e0ea213a81363aa7e9f4b0843988cfd6a958dea9d-4-1-credentials.png)  
**2\. Paste API key in your 91APP store’s settings**|  BotBonnie console| 91APP console| 

  1. In BotBonnie, click the copy icon for the **x-api-key** field.
  2. Switch to 91APP's console, navigate to the **Marketing Cloud** > Growth (**流量成長**) > LINE Module (**LINE 模組設定**), paste the copied key into the binding key field (**綁定鑰匙 Key**) and save your changes.   
  


![](https://files.readme.io/53abd49dd7365af44a641371e1f75990f01c54359f9b3d11336d1d8382e96238-step2.png)  
**3\. Input 91APP store’s binding link**|  91APP console| BotBonnie console| 

  1. In 91APP, copy the binding link (**綁定連結**) from its console.
  2. Switch to BotBonnie's console and paste the link into the **Binding link** field.   
  


![](https://files.readme.io/64cdad1b9bfa60ed727d22a8ccbe4b9756a929d20fe4249e54e6405a74851962-step3.png)  
**4\. Configure webhook URL in your 91APP store settings**|  BotBonnie console| 91APP console| 

  1. In BotBonnie, click the copy icon for the **Webhook URL**.
  2. Switch to 91APP's console and paste the URL into webhook URL (**綁定回傳網址 URL**) field and save your changes.   
  


![](https://files.readme.io/4e58b11de1e037c2b6cd6ecba0869ca7996935effe1e50aead153a78425fba4a-step4.png)  
  
  7. Go back to BotBonnie's console, click **Next** in the upper-right corner to continue to set up account linking.



* * *

## 

3\. (Optional) Set up account linking

Configure the following options:

  * Select the **Message of successful linking** checkbox and configure the confirmation message displayed when an account is successfully linked.
  * Select the **Switch menu group or triggered actions after account linking** checkbox to [trigger specific actions](doc:triggered-actions) after successful linking.

![](https://files.readme.io/6ea2eba6f3980a650cbbfb05af3f58794e983733dbdde87a03452606c4437ef6-91-set-up-account-linking.png)

__Updated 7 months ago

# Account Linking

After you've successfully [connected your e-commerce platform](doc:e-commerce-integrations), the next essential step is to enable account linking. This process establishes a secure connection between a user's chatbot identity and their e-commerce account, which is the key to unlocking a wide range of [personalized features](doc:e-commerce-integrations-features).

* * *

## 

How account linking works

Users link their e-commerce account to the chatbot in three steps:

  1. The user clicks an account linking button in your chatbot flow, LINE menu, or Messenger menu.
  2. Users verify their identity with a code sent by SMS or email, depending on your e-commerce integration settings.
  3. After verification, the user can access the personalized features.

![](https://files.readme.io/fbc9abc-account-linking-process.png)

* * *

## 

Set up an entry point for account linking

To get started, you can create entry points for account linking within your chatbot flow, LINE menu, or Messenger menu:

Entry point| Configuration steps  
---|---  
Chatbot flow| 

  1. Go to **Flow** and [add a button](doc:button#adding-a-button) to a module.   

  2. Under **On click** , select **Integrated EC service** > **Link the user 's account**.   
  


![](https://files.readme.io/1c5e69ba0234cbfb78b6c201eb00030677c28c82120ed9ba662f4e5fa5e09dbd-flow-account-linking.png)  
[LINE menu](doc:menus#line-menu-setup)| 

  1. In your LINE menu configuration, go to the **Image upload and layout settings**.   

  2. Under **Click the block** , select **Integrated EC service** > **Link the user 's account**.  
  


![](https://files.readme.io/a913994e814325100993ce43ceb332e25bd71868e87aac9e823372c805ca286c-line-menu-account-linking.png)  
[Messenger menu](doc:menus#messenger-menu-setup)| 

  1. First, [create a module](doc:modules-and-messages#adding-a-module) in your chatbot flow that includes the button for account linking.   

  2. Then, in your Messenger menu configuration, go to the **Persistent menu item settings** , click **\+ Add an item** , and select the flow module you created earlier.   
  


![](https://files.readme.io/e004c91bed1485d8241618c0cc3874cfe65c03609e13711b0f5ccf0fedb9340e-messenger-module.png)  
  
> 👍
> 
> ### 
> 
> Improve user experience
> 
> Enable these settings in your platform integration to enhance the account linking experience:
> 
>   * **Message of successful linking** : Automatically notify users when linking succeeds.
>   * **Switch menu group or triggered actions after account linking** : Change the user's menu or triggers specific flow after successful linking.
> 

> 
> Find these options in your platform integration guide:
> 
>   * [91APP integration](doc:e-commerce-integration-91app#3-optional-set-up-account-linking)
>   * [Cyberbiz integration](doc:e-commerce-integration-cyberbiz#3-set-up-account-linking)
>   * [Shopify integration](doc:e-commerce-integration-shopify#3-set-up-account-linking)
>   * [SHOPLINE integration](doc:e-commerce-integration-shopline#2-set-up-account-linking)
>

# Cyberbiz

> 👍
> 
> ### 
> 
> Supported channels
> 
>   * LINE
>   * Messenger
> 


Connect your Cyberbiz store to BotBonnie chatbot so users can view their shopping information and receive notifications through supported messaging channels. With this integration, users who link their accounts can:

  * View their [transaction history](doc:e-commerce-features-query-transaction-history)
  * Receive [automatic status notifications](doc:e-commerce-features-automatic-status-notifications)



Follow these setup steps to add a connection:

  1. Obtain credentials
  2. Connect with Cyberbiz
  3. Set up account linking
  4. (Optional) Set up notifications



After setup, you can [manage connections](doc:e-commerce-integrations#manage-connections) on whenever necessary.

* * *

## 

1\. Obtain credentials

Before you start the integration, you'll need your API credentials (username and secret) from Cyberbiz. Contact the Cyberbiz support team to request these credentials.

* * *

## 

2\. Connect with Cyberbiz

Follow these steps to connect your Cyberbiz store to BotBonnie.

  1. Open the project launcher dropdown in the top navigation bar, select **Common settings** , and then click **EC platforms** in the left navigation panel.

![](https://files.readme.io/42d9e57b124e8eaa22c136bae1bc157fed1c1f4b734c857cb4a8a5c4c63055d4-nav-ec-platforms.png)

  2. Click the **Cyberbiz** card to start the connection.

![](https://files.readme.io/a18235677105be340b546fa15b3a1698e325e6321554e64792bf5a0b20cdf230-cyberbiz-add-connection.png)

  3. Enter a name for your connection. Then, click **\+ Choose page(s)** to select the Facebook Messenger or LINE Official Account you want to connect.

![](https://files.readme.io/e9df42d556400f7d2d7877f1fdbe066da77e3bc650536b8754bf1b08d720529f-add-connection-choose-pages.png)

  4. Enter the following Cyberbiz credentials:
     * API username
     * API secret



> 📘
> 
> ### 
> 
> Note
> 
> Contact the Cyberbiz support team to get these API credentials.

  5. When you're done, select **Next** in the upper-right corner to continue to set up account linking.



* * *

## 

3\. Set up account linking

  1. Under **User login method** , select how users authenticate: **Phone number only** , **Email only** , or **Either phone number or email**.
  2. (Optional) Check the **Apply monthly SMS limit** box and specify the maximum number of SMS verification messages per month to control your budget.
  3. Enter a **Redirect URL** where users can register as members. This URL is essential for initiating the [account linking process](doc:e-commerce-integration-account-linking#set-up-an-entry-point-for-account-linking) via chatbot flows or menus.

![](https://files.readme.io/cf065652e2a5c60d05bb9cb32b0655031a6a0bb335a6bcb1f55514e711e649b2-redirect-url.png)

  4. Customize the **Terms and conditions** text to display during the account linking flow.
  5. Configure the following options:
     * **Message of successful linking** : Enable this to send a confirmation message when the system successfully links an account.
     * **Switch menu group or triggered actions after account linking** : Enable to [trigger specific actions](doc:triggered-actions) after successful linking.

![](https://files.readme.io/4dc824c5f30428586a566335a91594211af5103729b22681760ec93f24b854d1-account-linked-actions.png)

  6. When you're done, select **Next** in the upper-right corner to continue to set up notifications.



* * *

## 

4\. (Optional) Set up notifications

Enable automatic notifications to alert users about order, point and coupon updates to enhance user experience. For detailed configuration options, see [Automatic status notifications](doc:e-commerce-features-automatic-status-notifications).
